                <label><span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">素材包</span><select className="visual-native-select w-full" value={materialPackageId || ""} onChange={(event) => setMaterialPackageId(Number(event.target.value))}>{!availablePackages.length && <option value="">当前类目暂无素材包</option>}{availablePackages.map((item) => <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>{item.name} · {item.active_asset_count} 份</option>)}</select></label>
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-5">
              {currentForPackage ? (
                <Button onClick={() => setSearchParams({ run: String(currentForPackage.id) }, { replace: true })}>查看当前运行<ArrowRight /></Button>
              ) : (
                <Button disabled={!can(user, "production:rerun", categoryKey) || !allReady || createRun.isPending || Boolean(essentialError)} onClick={startRun}>{createRun.isPending ? <CircleNotch className="animate-spin" /> : <Play weight="fill" />}{createRun.isPending ? "正在开始" : "开始评测"}</Button>
              )}
              <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">离开页面不会中断任务；重复提交也不会重复建任务。</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <aside className="bg-alias-surface-044 px-5 py-6 md:px-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Lightbulb weight="fill" /><p className="text-xs font-bold">AI 下一步</p></div>
            <p className="mt-4 text-lg font-semibold leading-8">{operatorSafeText(focusedRun?.ai_next_step, allReady ? "条件已满足，可以开始评测。" : "请先完成左侧就绪检查。")}</p>
            <p className="mt-4 border-t border-alias-edge-045 pt-4 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-016">系统建议不会替代一审、二审或人工发布决定。</p>
          </aside>
        </section>

        <section>
          <SectionHeading title="开始前检查" description="普通审核员只需确认素材、类目和冻结方案三项。" />
          <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)] visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
            {readiness.map((item, index) => <div key={item.key} className="grid gap-3 px-5 py-4 sm:grid-cols-[36px_minmax(0,1fr)_auto] sm:items-center"><span className={`font-data flex size-8 items-center justify-center border rounded-md text-sm font-bold ${item.ready ? "border-alias-edge-007 bg-alias-surface-007 text-alias-content-001" : "border-alias-edge-053 bg-alias-surface-068 text-alias-content-033"}`}>{item.ready ? <Check weight="bold" /> : index + 1}</span><div><p className="text-sm font-semibold">{item.label}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{item.description}</p></div>{!item.ready && item.action_href && <Button asChild size="sm" variant="secondary"><Link to={item.action_href}>{item.action_label}<ArrowRight /></Link></Button>}</div>)}
          </div>
        </section>

        {focusedRun && (
          <section className="space-y-5">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4"><SectionHeading title="当前运行" description={`${focusedRun.material_package.name} · ${focusedRun.category.name}`} /><Button size="sm" variant="ghost" disabled={refreshFacts.isPending} onClick={() => refreshFacts.mutate()}><ArrowClockwise />刷新事实</Button></div>
            <ProductionTimeline steps={focusedRun.timeline} />
            <div className="visual-panel-group grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5">
              <Metric label="状态" value={productionStatusMeta[focusedRun.status].label} />
              <Metric label="总体进度" value={`${focusedRun.progress.percent}%`} />
              <Metric label="评测完成" value={`${focusedRun.job_counts.completed}/${focusedRun.job_counts.total}`} />
              <Metric label="待一审" value={String(focusedRun.pending_first_review_count)} />
              <Metric label="审计修订" value={String(focusedRun.audit.revision)} />
            </div>
            <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)] visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
              {focusedRun.blockers.length ? focusedRun.blockers.map((blocker) => <div key={blocker.code} className="grid gap-3 px-5 py-4 sm:grid-cols-[24px_minmax(0,1fr)_auto] sm:items-center"><WarningCircle className="text-warning" /><div><p className="text-sm font-semibold">{operatorSafeText(blocker.title, "需要处理")}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{operatorSafeText(blocker.message, "请打开对应页面处理后刷新。")}</p></div><Button asChild size="sm" variant="secondary"><Link to={blocker.fix.href}>{operatorSafeText(blocker.fix.label, "直接处理")}<ArrowRight /></Link></Button></div>) : <div className="flex items-start gap-3 px-5 py-5"><CheckCircle className="text-success" weight="fill" /><div><p className="text-sm font-semibold">当前没有人工阻塞</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">系统会按现有事实继续推进。</p></div></div>}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        <section>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4"><SectionHeading title="最近生产运行" description="这里展示生产过程；二审最终包在独立列表中查看。" /><Badge>{user.is_admin ? "管理员" : "审核员"}</Badge></div>
          <ProductionRunRows items={runs.data?.items ?? []} loading={runs.isLoading} onFocus={(id) => setSearchParams({ run: String(id) }, { replace: true })} />
        </section>
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

export function EvaluationPackageReviewListPage() {
  const [showHistory, setShowHistory] = useState(false)
  const packages = useQuery({ queryKey: ["evaluation-packages"], queryFn: evaluationPackageApi.list, retry: false, refetchInterval: 5000 })
  const items = packages.data?.items ?? []
  const pending = items.filter((item) => item.status === "awaiting_review")
  const history = items.filter((item) => ["approved", "rejected", "published", "archived"].includes(item.status))
  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader index="03.1" title="二审评测包" description="这里只展示已经冻结的最终评测包。按变更、维度、黄金集、回归失败项和风险顺序完成一次整体判断。" actions={<Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => packages.refetch()}><ArrowClockwise />刷新队列</Button>} />
      <div className="mx-auto shell-content px-page py-8">
        <div className="visual-panel-group mb-6 grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-3"><QueueMetric label="等待二审" value={pending.length} active={!showHistory} onClick={() => setShowHistory(false)} /><QueueMetric label="已有结论" value={history.length} active={showHistory} onClick={() => setShowHistory(true)} /><div className="bg-alias-surface-044 px-5 py-4"><p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">发布门禁</p><p className="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold">批准与发布始终是两个动作</p></div></div>
        {packages.error ? <OperatorErrorPanel error={toOperatorError(packages.error)} onRetry={() => packages.refetch()} /> : <EvaluationPackageRows items={showHistory ? history : pending} loading={packages.isLoading} />}
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

type ReviewAction = "approve" | "reject" | "publish" | "archive"

export function EvaluationPackageDetailPage() {
  const canManage = useAccess().can("*")
  const { packageId } = useParams()
  const id = Number(packageId)
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const [note, setNote] = useState("")
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState<OperatorError | null>(null)
  const detail = useQuery({ queryKey: ["evaluation-package", id], queryFn: () => evaluationPackageApi.get(id), enabled: Number.isInteger(id) && id > 0, retry: false, refetchInterval: (query) => query.state.data?.status === "validating" ? 4000 : false })
  const action = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ type, value }: { type: ReviewAction; value: string }) => type === "approve" ? evaluationPackageApi.approve(id, value) : type === "reject" ? evaluationPackageApi.reject(id, value) : type === "publish" ? evaluationPackageApi.publish(id, value) : evaluationPackageApi.archive(id, value),
    onSuccess: async (updated, variables) => {
      setNote("")
      setActionError(null)
      queryClient.setQueryData(["evaluation-package", id], updated)
      await Promise.all([queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["evaluation-packages"] }), queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["evaluation-production-runs"] })])
      toast.success({ approve: "评测包已批准，仍需明确发布", reject: "评测包已拒绝", publish: "评测包已发布", archive: "评测包已归档" }[variables.type])
    },
    onError: (error) => setActionError(toOperatorError(error)),
  })
  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) return <InvalidPackage onBack={() => navigate("/workflow/releases/packages", { replace: true })} />
  if (detail.isLoading) return <DetailLoading />
  if (detail.error || !detail.data) return <><PageHeader index="03.2" title="二审评测包详情" description="读取冻结证据。" /><div className="mx-auto shell-content px-page py-10"><OperatorErrorPanel error={toOperatorError(detail.error)} onRetry={() => detail.refetch()} /></div></>
  const item = detail.data
  const status = packageStatusMeta(item.status)
  const canReview = canManage && item.status === "awaiting_review"
  const canPublish = canManage && item.status === "approved"
  const canArchive = canManage && ["approved", "rejected", "published"].includes(item.status)
  const noteReady = Boolean(note.trim())
  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader index="03.2" title={`二审评测包 · ${item.category_key}`} description={`${status.label} · 创建于 ${formatTime(item.created_at)}`} actions={<><Button asChild variant="secondary"><Link to="/workflow/releases/packages"><ArrowLeft />返回二审队列</Link></Button><Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => detail.refetch()}><ArrowClockwise />刷新</Button></>} />
      <div className="mx-auto shell-content space-y-8 px-page py-8">
        {actionError && <OperatorErrorPanel error={actionError} onRetry={() => detail.refetch()} onClose={() => setActionError(null)} />}
        <section className="visual-panel-group grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4"><Metric label="状态" value={status.label} /><Metric label="评测模式" value={item.prompts.mode === "single" ? "单次完整评测" : "A/B 两段评测"} /><Metric label="黄金样本" value={String(item.golden_sample_set.item_count)} /><Metric label="回归结果" value={regressionLabel(item.regression.recommendation)} /></section>

        <ReviewDecisionBrief item={item} />

        <section><SectionHeading title="完整提示词" description="展示最终包冻结的完整 A/B 或单提示词内容，不展示内部编号。" /><div className="space-y-5"><PromptSnapshot title={item.prompts.mode === "single" ? "单次完整评测" : "A 阶段 · 分类与画质"} prompt={item.prompts.a} />{item.prompts.b && <PromptSnapshot title="B 阶段 · 美感维度" prompt={item.prompts.b} />}</div></section>

        <DimensionPlan item={item} />

        <GoldenSampleEvidence item={item} />

        <RegressionEvidence item={item} />

        <ManifestSection title="自动改进记录" description="候选来源、关联回归和不可自动发布声明。">{item.automation ? <EvidenceObject value={hideInternalPromptIds(item.automation)} /> : <MissingEvidence text="本评测包未关联自动改进运行。" />}</ManifestSection>
        <ManifestSection title="指标快照" description="回归指标、摘要与版本指标快照。"><EvidenceObject value={item.metrics} /></ManifestSection>
        <ManifestSection title="身份与版本" description="模型、评分规则、引擎和候选策略身份。" advanced><EvidenceObject value={item.identity} /></ManifestSection>

        <section className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
          <div className="border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-5"><div className="flex items-center gap-2"><ShieldCheck /><h2 className="visual-card-title">二审与发布</h2></div><p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">批准不会自动发布；拒绝、发布和归档都保留冻结证据。</p></div>
          <div className="grid gap-4 px-5 py-5 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto] lg:items-end"><label><span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">{canArchive && !canReview && !canPublish ? "归档原因" : "决定说明"}</span><Textarea rows={4} maxLength={4000} value={note} onChange={(event) => setNote(event.target.value)} /></label><div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">{canReview && <><Button variant="danger" disabled={!noteReady || action.isPending} onClick={() => action.mutate({ type: "reject", value: note.trim() })}>{action.variables?.type === "reject" && action.isPending ? <CircleNotch className="animate-spin" /> : <XCircle />}拒绝</Button><Button disabled={!noteReady || action.isPending} onClick={() => action.mutate({ type: "approve", value: note.trim() })}>{action.variables?.type === "approve" && action.isPending ? <CircleNotch className="animate-spin" /> : <CheckCircle />}批准</Button></>}{canPublish && <Button disabled={!noteReady || action.isPending} onClick={() => action.mutate({ type: "publish", value: note.trim() })}><Play weight="fill" />明确发布</Button>}{canArchive && <Button variant="secondary" disabled={!noteReady || action.isPending} onClick={() => action.mutate({ type: "archive", value: note.trim() })}><Archive />归档</Button>}{!canReview && !canPublish && !canArchive && <Badge tone={status.tone}>{status.label}</Badge>}</div></div>
          {item.review.decision && <div className="border-t border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-5 py-4 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">二审结论：{item.review.decision === "approved" ? "批准" : "拒绝"} · {item.review.reviewed_by || "未记录"} · {formatTime(item.review.reviewed_at)}<br />{item.review.note}</div>}
        </section>
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

const operatorJourneySteps = [
  { label: "导入素材", note: "生成可追溯素材包", to: "/workflow/materials/packages" },
  { label: "选择素材包与类目", note: "确认本次评测来源", to: "/workflow/production-line" },
  { label: "开始评测", note: "系统自动运行并留痕", to: "/workflow/production-line" },
  { label: "处理纠偏", note: "只处理需要人工判断的结果", to: "/workflow/review/low-confidence" },
  { label: "二审评测包", note: "整体判断冻结方案与证据", to: "/workflow/releases/packages" },
] as const

function journeyStep(run: EvaluationProductionRun | undefined, hasMaterials: boolean) {
  if (!hasMaterials) return 1
  if (!run) return 2
  if (["awaiting_review", "approved", "rejected", "published", "archived"].includes(run.status)) return 5
  if (run.status === "first_review" || run.current_stage.includes("review")) return 4
  return 3
}

function OperatorJourney({ current }: { current: number }) {
  return (
    <section aria-label="一线审核主流程">
      <SectionHeading title="本次工作到哪一步" description="默认只沿这条主线推进；模型、预算、协议和执行器由管理员在高级设置中维护。" />
      <ol className="visual-panel-group grid overflow-hidden visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5">
        {operatorJourneySteps.map((step, index) => {
          const number = index + 1
          const completed = number < current
          const active = number === current
          return (
            <li key={step.label} className={`min-w-0 border-b border-r border-[var(--line)] lg:border-b-0 ${active ? "bg-alias-surface-044" : "bg-white"}`}>
              <Link className="block min-h-28 px-4 py-4 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-primary" to={step.to}>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className={`font-data flex size-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center border rounded-md text-xs font-bold ${completed ? "border-alias-edge-007 bg-alias-surface-007 text-alias-content-001" : active ? "border-alias-edge-011 bg-primary" : "border-[var(--line-strong)] text-[var(--muted)]"}`}>
                    {completed ? <Check weight="bold" /> : number}
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold">{step.label}</p>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{step.note}</p>
              </Link>
            </li>
          )
        })}
      </ol>
    </section>
  )
}

function ReviewDecisionBrief({ item }: { item: EvaluationPackageDetail }) {
  const blockers: string[] = []
  const risks: string[] = []
  if (!item.manifest_hash_valid) blockers.push("冻结清单校验未通过，不能据此作出发布决定")
  if (!item.regression.terminal || item.regression.completed < item.regression.total) blockers.push(`黄金集回归尚未完成（${item.regression.completed}/${item.regression.total}）`)
  if (!item.golden_sample_set.item_count) blockers.push("评测包没有冻结黄金样本")
  if (item.regression.recommendation === "fail") risks.push("回归门禁建议拒绝，必须逐项检查失败样本")
  if (item.regression.failed > 0) risks.push(`${item.regression.failed} 个样本未通过回归对照`)
  if (item.golden_sample_set.judgable_item_count < item.golden_sample_set.item_count) risks.push(`${item.golden_sample_set.item_count - item.golden_sample_set.judgable_item_count} 个黄金样本缺少可判断真值`)
  if (!item.prompts.a.change_note?.trim() && !item.prompts.b?.change_note?.trim() && !item.change_summary.trim()) risks.push("提示词版本没有填写明确的变更说明")

  const nextStep = blockers.length
    ? "先处理阻塞项，不要批准。刷新后确认冻结证据完整。"
    : item.regression.recommendation === "fail" || item.regression.failed > 0
      ? "先查看下方失败项和基线—候选差异；无法解释或不可接受时拒绝本包。"
      : item.status === "approved"
        ? "本包已通过二审；如确认要进入正式标签，再单独执行发布。"
        : "依次核对提示词变更、维度开关、黄金集组成和逐样本对照，再填写二审决定。"

  return (
    <section>
      <SectionHeading title="二审判断摘要" description="先看阻塞和风险，再展开完整证据。AI 只给下一步建议，不替你作决定。" />
      <div className="visual-panel-group grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-[var(--line)] lg:grid-cols-3">
        <div className="min-w-0 bg-alias-surface-044 px-5 py-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Lightbulb weight="fill" /><h3 className="text-sm font-bold">AI 下一步建议</h3></div>
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold leading-6">{nextStep}</p>
          {(item.ai.change_summary || item.change_summary) && <p className="mt-3 border-t border-alias-edge-045 pt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-016">变更摘要：{item.ai.change_summary || item.change_summary}</p>}
        </div>
        <div className={`min-w-0 px-5 py-5 ${blockers.length ? "bg-alias-surface-066" : "bg-white"}`}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Prohibit /><h3 className="text-sm font-bold">阻塞</h3><Badge tone={blockers.length ? "danger" : "success"}>{blockers.length ? `${blockers.length} 项` : "无"}</Badge></div>
          {blockers.length ? <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-xs leading-5">{blockers.map((text) => <li key={text}>· {text}</li>)}</ul> : <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">冻结清单和回归完成度满足二审前置条件。</p>}
        </div>
        <div className={`min-w-0 px-5 py-5 ${risks.length ? "bg-alias-surface-076" : "bg-white"}`}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><ShieldWarning /><h3 className="text-sm font-bold">风险</h3><Badge tone={risks.length ? "warning" : "success"}>{risks.length ? `${risks.length} 项` : "低"}</Badge></div>
          {risks.length ? <ul className="mt-3 space-y-2 text-xs leading-5">{risks.map((text) => <li key={text}>· {text}</li>)}</ul> : <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">当前未发现由冻结证据直接暴露的风险项。</p>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

type JsonRecord = Record<string, unknown>

function asRecord(value: unknown): JsonRecord | null {
  return value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? value as JsonRecord : null
}

function asRecordArray(value: unknown) {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(asRecord).filter((item): item is JsonRecord => Boolean(item)) : []
}

function findDimensionDefinition(value: unknown, depth = 0): JsonRecord | null {
  if (depth > 5) return null
  const record = asRecord(value)
  if (!record) return null
  if (asRecordArray(record.dimensions).length) return record
  for (const child of Object.values(record)) {
    const found = findDimensionDefinition(child, depth + 1)
    if (found) return found
  }
  return null
}

function DimensionPlan({ item }: { item: EvaluationPackageDetail }) {
  const dimensions = asRecord(item.dimensions) ?? {}
  const explicitSchema = asRecord(dimensions.explicit_schema)
  const definition = findDimensionDefinition(explicitSchema?.definition) ?? findDimensionDefinition(dimensions)
  const rows = asRecordArray(definition?.dimensions)
  const profile = asRecord(item.category.profile)
  const pipeline = asRecord(profile?.pipeline_config)
  const switchConfig = asRecord(pipeline?.dimensions)
  const enabled = switchConfig?.enabled !== false
  const mode = switchConfig?.mode === "selected" ? "仅重点维度" : "全部维度"
  const route = asRecord(dimensions.explicit_route_policy)
  const schemaVersion = String(explicitSchema?.version ?? profile?.dimension_schema_version ?? dimensions.resolved_schema_contract_version ?? "未记录")
  const routeVersion = String(route?.version ?? "跟随冻结方案")
  return (
    <section>
      <SectionHeading title="维度规则与开关" description="二审查看本包真正冻结的规则，不受管理员之后修改影响。" />
      <div className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
        <div className="grid gap-px bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <Metric label="维度开关" value={enabled ? "已启用" : "已关闭"} />
          <Metric label="评测范围" value={mode} />
          <Metric label="维度版本" value={schemaVersion} />
          <Metric label="路由版本" value={routeVersion} />
        </div>
        {rows.length ? (
          <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)]">
            {rows.map((dimension, index) => {
              const anchors = asRecord(dimension.anchors)
              const weight = typeof dimension.weight === "number" ? `${Math.round(dimension.weight * 100)}%` : "未设权重"
              return (
                <div key={String(dimension.key ?? index)} className="grid gap-3 px-5 py-4 md:grid-cols-[minmax(180px,0.8fr)_110px_minmax(0,1.4fr)] md:items-start">
                  <div><p className="text-sm font-bold">{String(dimension.label ?? dimension.key ?? `维度 ${index + 1}`)}</p><p className="font-data mt-1 break-all text-xs text-[var(--muted)]"><span className="visual-code">{String(dimension.key ?? "")}</span></p></div>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2"><Badge tone={dimension.required === false ? "neutral" : "success"}>{dimension.required === false ? "可选" : "必评"}</Badge><Badge>{weight}</Badge></div>
                  <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">中位标准：{String(anchors?.["3"] ?? "未记录等级锚点")}</p>
                </div>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        ) : <MissingEvidence text="本包保留了维度身份，但未提供可逐项展示的冻结定义。" />}
        <details className="visual-native-details border-t border-[var(--line)]"><summary className="font-bold">查看维度冻结原始记录</summary><div className="border-t border-[var(--line)] p-5"><EvidenceObject value={item.dimensions} /></div></details>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

function GoldenSampleEvidence({ item }: { item: EvaluationPackageDetail }) {
  const roles = item.golden_sample_set.items.reduce<Record<string, number>>((counts, sample) => {
    const key = sample.role || "unassigned"
    counts[key] = (counts[key] ?? 0) + 1
    return counts
  }, {})
  return (
    <section>
      <SectionHeading title="黄金集组成与样本" description={`${item.golden_sample_set.name} · ${item.golden_sample_set.item_count} 个样本 · 可判断 ${item.golden_sample_set.judgable_item_count}/${item.golden_sample_set.item_count}`} />
      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">{Object.entries(roles).map(([role, count]) => <Badge key={role}>{roleLabel(role)} {count}</Badge>)}</div>
      <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)] visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
        {item.golden_sample_set.items.map((sample) => <div key={sample.sample_item_id} className="grid min-w-0 gap-4 px-5 py-4 sm:grid-cols-[72px_minmax(0,1fr)] lg:grid-cols-[72px_minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(220px,0.6fr)] lg:items-center"><img src={sample.image_url} alt={sample.asset_name} className="size-16 border border-[var(--line)] bg-white object-cover" /><div className="min-w-0"><p className="file-name break-all text-sm">{sample.asset_name}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">人工真值 {sample.expected_level || "未记录"} · {roleLabel(sample.role || "unassigned")} · 真值修订 {sample.truth_revision}</p></div><details className="visual-native-details min-w-0 sm:col-start-2 lg:col-start-auto"><summary className="font-bold">查看样本真值详情</summary><div className="mt-3 max-w-full"><EvidenceObject value={sample.truth} compact /></div></details></div>)}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

function RegressionEvidence({ item }: { item: EvaluationPackageDetail }) {
  const summary = asRecord(item.regression.summary)
  const rules = asRecord(item.regression.metric_rules)
  const thresholds = asRecord(rules?.thresholds)
  const gates = asRecordArray(summary?.gate_checks)
  const sortedItems = [...item.regression.items].sort((left, right) => Number(right.passed === false) - Number(left.passed === false))
  return (
    <section>
      <SectionHeading title="回归规则、对照与失败项" description={`${item.regression.completed}/${item.regression.total} 已完成 · ${regressionLabel(item.regression.recommendation)} · 规则版本 ${item.regression.metric_rules_version || "未记录"}`} />
      <div className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
        <div className="grid gap-px bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4"><Metric label="通过" value={String(item.regression.passed)} /><Metric label="失败" value={String(item.regression.failed)} /><Metric label="门槛" value={formatThreshold(item.regression.threshold)} /><Metric label="运行状态" value={regressionStatusLabel(item.regression.status)} /></div>
        <div className="grid gap-px border-t border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--line)] lg:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="min-w-0 bg-white px-5 py-5"><h3 className="text-sm font-bold">指标门槛</h3>{thresholds && Object.keys(thresholds).length ? <dl className="mt-3 space-y-2">{Object.entries(thresholds).map(([key, value]) => <div key={key} className="grid gap-1 text-xs sm:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto]"><dt className="break-words text-[var(--muted)]">{humanizeEvidenceKey(key)}</dt><dd className="font-data font-bold">{formatEvidenceValue(value)}</dd></div>)}</dl> : <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">没有单独记录指标门槛。</p>}</div>
          <div className="min-w-0 bg-white px-5 py-5"><h3 className="text-sm font-bold">门禁检查</h3>{gates.length ? <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">{gates.map((gate, index) => <div key={`${String(gate.gate)}-${index}`} className="flex min-w-0 items-start justify-between gap-3 text-xs"><span className="min-w-0 break-words text-[var(--muted)]">{humanizeEvidenceKey(String(gate.gate ?? `检查 ${index + 1}`))}</span><Badge tone={gate.passed === true ? "success" : "danger"}>{gate.passed === true ? "通过" : "失败"}</Badge></div>)}</div> : <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">没有逐条门禁结果。</p>}</div>
        </div>
        <div className="border-t border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4"><div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2"><h3 className="text-sm font-bold">逐样本回归对照</h3><p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">失败项优先排列；基线是旧方案，候选是本次新方案。</p></div></div>
        <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)]">
          {sortedItems.length ? sortedItems.map((entry, index) => <RegressionItemRow key={String(entry.id ?? index)} entry={entry} />) : <MissingEvidence text="本次回归没有冻结逐样本对照。" />}
        </div>
        <details className="visual-native-details border-t border-[var(--line)]"><summary className="font-bold">查看完整冻结回归记录</summary><div className="border-t border-[var(--line)] p-5"><EvidenceObject value={{ metrics: item.regression.metrics, summary: item.regression.summary, metric_rules: item.regression.metric_rules, items: item.regression.items }} /></div></details>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

function RegressionItemRow({ entry }: { entry: JsonRecord }) {
  const comparison = asRecord(entry.comparison)
  const diffs = Array.isArray(comparison?.diffs) ? comparison.diffs : []
  const failed = entry.passed === false || entry.status === "error"
  return (
    <div className={`grid min-w-0 gap-4 px-5 py-4 sm:grid-cols-[72px_minmax(0,1fr)] lg:grid-cols-[72px_minmax(180px,0.7fr)_minmax(0,1fr)_auto] lg:items-center ${failed ? "bg-alias-surface-073" : "bg-white"}`}>
      {typeof entry.image_url === "string" ? <HoverPreview src={entry.image_url} alt={String(entry.asset_name ?? "回归样本")}><img src={entry.image_url} alt={String(entry.asset_name ?? "回归样本")} className="size-16 border border-[var(--line)] bg-white object-cover" /></HoverPreview> : <div className="flex size-16 items-center justify-center border border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 rounded-md"><Images /></div>}
      <div className="min-w-0"><p className="file-name break-all text-sm">{String(entry.asset_name ?? `样本 ${entry.sample_item_id ?? ""}`)}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{roleLabel(String(entry.sample_role ?? "unassigned"))}</p></div>
      <div className="min-w-0 text-xs leading-5"><p><span className="text-[var(--muted)]">基线：</span>{extractResultLevel(entry.baseline_result)}</p><p><span className="text-[var(--muted)]">候选：</span>{extractResultLevel(entry.candidate_result)}</p><p className="mt-1 break-words text-[var(--muted)]">差异：{diffs.length ? diffs.map(formatDiff).join("；") : "没有记录字段差异"}</p></div>
      <Badge tone={failed ? "danger" : entry.passed === true ? "success" : "warning"}>{failed ? "失败项" : entry.passed === true ? "通过" : "待完成"}</Badge>
    </div>
  )
}

function roleLabel(role: string) {
  return ({ target_error: "目标错例", stable_control: "稳定对照", blind_holdout: "盲测样本", unassigned: "未分组" } as Record<string, string>)[role] ?? role
}

function extractResultLevel(value: unknown) {
  const record = asRecord(value)
  const fields = asRecord(record?.fields)
  return String(fields?.level ?? record?.level ?? "未记录等级")
}

function formatDiff(value: unknown) {
  const record = asRecord(value)
  if (!record) return formatEvidenceValue(value)
  const field = humanizeEvidenceKey(String(record.field ?? "字段"))
  return `${field}：${formatEvidenceValue(record.change ?? record.value ?? "有变化")}`
}

function humanizeEvidenceKey(value: string) {
  const known: Record<string, string> = {
    all: "全部门禁",
    level_consistency_max_drop: "等级一致率最大允许下降",
    release_gate_passed: "发布门禁",
    target_error_recovery: "目标错例修复",
    critical_regressions: "关键字段退化",
    new_severe_errors: "新增严重错误",
  }
  return known[value] ?? value.replaceAll("_", " ")
}

function formatEvidenceValue(value: unknown) {
  if (value === true) return "是"
  if (value === false) return "否"
  if (value == null) return "未记录"
  if (typeof value === "number") return Number.isInteger(value) ? String(value) : value.toFixed(3).replace(/0+$/, "").replace(/\.$/, "")
  if (typeof value === "string") return value
  return JSON.stringify(value)
}

function formatThreshold(value: number) {
  return value >= 0 && value <= 1 ? `${Math.round(value * 100)}%` : String(value)
}

function regressionStatusLabel(value: string) {
  return ({ passed: "已通过", regressed: "已完成未通过", waiting_results: "等待结果", running: "正在运行", failed: "运行失败" } as Record<string, string>)[value] ?? value
}

function PromptSnapshot({ title, prompt }: { title: string; prompt: EvaluationPackageDetail["prompts"]["a"] }) {
  return <article className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white"><div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4"><div><p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{title}</p><h3 className="mt-1 text-lg font-bold">{prompt.name}</h3><p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">本版变更：{prompt.change_note?.trim() || "该版本没有单独填写变更说明"}</p></div><div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2"><Badge tone="active">版本 {prompt.version}</Badge><Badge>规则 {prompt.rubric_version}</Badge></div></div><div className="grid gap-px bg-[var(--line)] lg:grid-cols-2"><PromptBlock label="系统指令全文" value={prompt.system_prompt} /><PromptBlock label="用户指令全文" value={prompt.user_prompt} /></div></article>
}

function PromptBlock({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return <div className="min-w-0 bg-white"><p className="px-5 pt-4 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p><pre className="max-h-[520px] overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap break-words px-5 py-4 font-data text-xs leading-6">{value || "未提供"}</pre></div>
}

function ManifestSection({ title, description, children, advanced = false }: { title: string; description: string; children: React.ReactNode; advanced?: boolean }) {
  if (advanced) return <details className="visual-native-details visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white"><summary className="flex items-center gap-2 font-bold"><LockKey />{title}</summary><div className="border-t border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-5"><p className="mb-4 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{description}</p>{children}</div></details>
  return <section><SectionHeading title={title} description={description} /><div className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white p-5">{children}</div></section>
}

function EvidenceObject({ value, compact = false }: { value: unknown; compact?: boolean }) {
  return <pre className={`${compact ? "max-h-56" : "max-h-[680px]"} overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap break-words bg-alias-surface-051 p-4 font-data text-xs leading-6`}>{JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}</pre>
}

function ProductionRunRows({ items, loading, onFocus }: { items: EvaluationProductionRun[]; loading: boolean; onFocus: (id: number) => void }) {
  if (loading) return <div className="h-48 animate-pulse visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white" />
  if (!items.length) return <EmptyState title="还没有生产运行" />
  return <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)] visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">{items.map((item) => { const meta = productionStatusMeta[item.status]; return <button key={item.id} type="button" className="visual-native-button grid w-full gap-4 px-5 py-4 text-left hover:bg-alias-surface-047 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(220px,1fr)_minmax(180px,0.8fr)_180px_130px_auto] xl:items-center" onClick={() => onFocus(item.id)}><div className="min-w-0"><p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold">{item.material_package.name}</p><p className="font-data mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">运行 <span className="visual-code">#{item.id}</span> · {formatTime(item.updated_at)}</p></div><div><p className="text-sm">{item.category.name}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{item.job_counts.total} 份素材</p></div><div><div className="h-1.5 bg-alias-surface-009"><div className="h-full bg-primary" style={{ width: `${item.progress.percent}%` }} /></div><p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{item.progress.current_step}</p></div><Badge tone={meta.tone}>{meta.label}</Badge><ArrowRight /></button>})}</div>
}

function EvaluationPackageRows({ items, loading }: { items: EvaluationPackageSummary[]; loading: boolean }) {
  if (loading) return <div className="h-48 animate-pulse visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white" />
  if (!items.length) return <EmptyState title="当前没有评测包" />
  return <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)] visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">{items.map((item) => { const meta = packageStatusMeta(item.status); return <Link key={item.id} to={`/workflow/releases/packages/${item.id}`} className="grid gap-4 px-5 py-4 hover:bg-alias-surface-047 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(220px,1fr)_minmax(180px,0.8fr)_140px_auto] lg:items-center"><div><p className="text-sm font-semibold">评测包 <span className="visual-code">#{item.id}</span></p><p className="visual-code mt-1 truncate text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{item.package_key}</p></div><div><p className="text-sm">{item.category_key}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{item.prompt_mode === "single" ? "单次完整评测" : "A/B 两段评测"}</p></div><Badge tone={meta.tone}>{meta.label}</Badge><ArrowRight /></Link>})}</div>
}

function ProductionTimeline({ steps }: { steps: EvaluationProductionTimelineStep[] }) {
  return <ol className="visual-panel-group grid overflow-hidden visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-7">{steps.map((step, index) => <li key={step.key} className={`min-w-0 border-b border-r border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-4 lg:border-b-0 ${step.status === "current" ? "bg-alias-surface-044" : step.status === "blocked" || step.status === "failed" ? "bg-alias-surface-068" : "bg-white"}`}><div className="flex items-center gap-2"><span className={`font-data flex size-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center border rounded-md text-xs font-bold ${step.status === "completed" ? "border-alias-edge-007 bg-alias-surface-007 text-alias-content-001" : step.status === "current" ? "border-alias-edge-011 bg-primary" : "border-[var(--line-strong)] text-[var(--muted)]"}`}>{step.status === "completed" ? <Check weight="bold" /> : index + 1}</span><p className="text-sm font-semibold">{step.label}</p></div>{step.completed_at && <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{formatTime(step.completed_at)}</p>}</li>)}</ol>
}

function OperatorErrorPanel({ error, compact = false, onRetry, onClose }: { error: OperatorError; compact?: boolean; onRetry?: () => void; onClose?: () => void }) {
  return <div className={`border-y border-alias-edge-056 bg-alias-surface-066 text-alias-content-032 ${compact ? "px-4 py-3" : "px-5 py-5"}`} role="alert"><div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4"><div className="flex gap-3"><WarningCircle className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" weight="fill" /><div><p className="text-sm font-bold">{error.title}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5">{error.message}</p></div></div><div className="flex gap-2">{onRetry && error.retryable && <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={onRetry}><ArrowClockwise />重试</Button>}{onClose && <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>关闭</Button>}</div></div></div>
}

function SectionHeading({ title, description }: { title: string; description: string }) { return <div className="mb-4"><h2 className="visual-card-title">{title}</h2><p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">{description}</p></div> }
function Metric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) { return <div className="min-w-0 bg-white px-5 py-4"><p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p><p className="font-data mt-2 truncate text-xl font-bold" title={value}>{value}</p></div> }
function QueueMetric({ label, value, active, onClick }: { label: string; value: number; active: boolean; onClick: () => void }) { return <button type="button" className={"visual-native-button block " + (`px-5 py-4 text-left ${active ? "bg-primary" : "bg-white"}`)} onClick={onClick}><p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p><p className="font-data mt-2 text-2xl font-bold">{value}</p></button> }
function MissingEvidence({ text }: { text: string }) { return <div className="flex items-start gap-3 bg-alias-surface-076 px-4 py-4 text-xs text-alias-content-027"><WarningCircle /><p>{text}</p></div> }
function EmptyState({ title }: { title: string }) { return <div className="flex min-h-48 flex-col items-center justify-center visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white"><Package size={30} weight="light" /><h3 className="visual-card-title mt-4">{title}</h3></div> }
function DetailLoading() { return <><PageHeader index="03.2" title="正在打开评测包" description="读取冻结证据。" /><div className="mx-auto shell-content px-page py-8"><div className="h-72 animate-pulse bg-white" /></div></> }
function InvalidPackage({ onBack }: { onBack: () => void }) { return <><PageHeader index="03.2" title="无法打开评测包" description="链接中的编号无效。" /><div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[50dvh] flex-col items-center justify-center"><FileText size={32} /><Button className="mt-5" onClick={onBack}><ArrowLeft />返回二审队列</Button></div></> }
function regressionLabel(value: string) { return value === "pass" ? "建议通过" : value === "fail" ? "建议拒绝" : "尚未形成" }
function formatTime(value: string | null | undefined) { return formatPlatformTime(value, "时间未记录") }
function hideInternalPromptIds(value: unknown): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(hideInternalPromptIds)
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return value
  return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value).filter(([key]) => !["prompt_id", "prompt_ids", "prompt_a_id", "prompt_b_id"].includes(key)).map(([key, item]) => [key, hideInternalPromptIds(item)]))
}

===== FILE frontend/src/pages/failure-console-page.tsx =====
import { useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react"
import { Link } from "react-router-dom"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { ArrowClockwise } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell"
import { AssetThumbnail } from "@/components/asset-thumbnail"
import { ImageLightbox, type ImagePreview } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Pager } from "@/components/ui/pager"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"
import { failureApi, type FailureRow, type ResolutionFields } from "@/features/failure-console/api"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"
import { createIdempotencyToken } from "@/lib/idempotency-token"
import { InvocationReviewPanel } from "@/features/failure-console/invocation-review-panel"
import type { EvaluationCategoryProfile } from "@/lib/types"
import { formatPlatformTime } from "@/lib/platform-time"
import { useAccess } from "@/lib/access"

const categoryLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  knowledge_graph: "知识图谱",
  inspiration_image: "灵感图",
  model_3d_su: "3D & SU",
  space_image: "空间图片",
  material_image: "材质图",
  design_case: "设计案例",
}

const FIELD_LABELS: Array<[keyof ResolutionFields, string]> = [
  ["title", "标题"],
  ["seotitle", "SEO 标题"],
  ["category", "分类"],
  ["style", "风格"],
  ["cons", "不足"],
  ["design", "设计说明"],
]

const INSPIRATION_FIELD_LABELS: Array<[keyof ResolutionFields, string]> = [
  ["title", "标题"], ["seo_title", "SEO 标题"], ["class_text", "分类（一级-二级）"],
  ["style", "风格"], ["cons", "不足"], ["design", "设计说明"],
  ["reason", "下游判断理由"], ["trait", "图片特征"], ["image_defects", "画面缺陷"],
]

const PAGE_SIZE = 50

/** 失败处理：跑挂的素材逐行摆开，人来决定重试还是直接给结论。
 *
 *  和运行中心那张卡的区别：那边管的是调度条目与投递事件，这里一行一条**素材**，
 *  带全它的身份、卡在哪一步、工厂说了什么。人工结论不改模型事实——DAG 上这条 run
 *  仍然是失败的，结论单独记，投下游的那一版自证是人填的。 */
export function FailureConsolePage() {
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const [offset, setOffset] = useState(0)
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("")
  const [searchInput, setSearchInput] = useState("")
  const [resolved, setResolved] = useState<boolean | null>(false)
  const [openRun, setOpenRun] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [categoryKey, setCategoryKey] = useState("")
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<ImagePreview | null>(null)
  const categories = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["evaluation-categories"],
    queryFn: () => api<{ items: EvaluationCategoryProfile[] }>("/api/evaluation-categories"),
  })
  const categoryOptions = categories.data?.items.map((category) => [category.category_key, category.display_name])
    ?? Object.entries(categoryLabels)

  const failures = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["failures", offset, search, resolved, categoryKey],
    queryFn: () => failureApi.list({ resolved, search, category_key: categoryKey, limit: PAGE_SIZE, offset }),
  })

  const refresh = () => client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["failures"] })
  const rows = failures.data?.items ?? []
  const total = failures.data?.total ?? 0

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <PageHeader
        index="02.6"
        title="失败处理"
        description="跑挂的素材停在这里等人。可以按运行重试（只重跑失败那一步），也可以直接给出最终结论——结论会带着人填的字段投一版下游，并且始终标明是人给的，不会伪装成模型结果。"
        actions={(
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => refresh()} disabled={failures.isFetching}>
            <ArrowClockwise />刷新
          </Button>
        )}
      />

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-5 py-4 rounded-md">
        <div>
          <label className="text-xs font-bold" htmlFor="failure-category">类目</label>
          <select id="failure-category" className="visual-native-select w-auto max-w-full mt-2 block"
            value={categoryKey} onChange={(event) => { setCategoryKey(event.target.value); setOffset(0); setOpenRun(null) }}>
            <option value="">全部类目</option>
            {categoryOptions.map(([key, label]) => <option key={key} value={key}>{label}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
        <form
          className="flex items-end gap-2"
          onSubmit={(event) => { event.preventDefault(); setOffset(0); setSearch(searchInput) }}
        >
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-bold" htmlFor="failure-search">按 ID 搜索</label>
            <Input
              id="failure-search"
              className="mt-2 h-11 min-w-56"
              placeholder="运行号 / task / img / ll"
              value={searchInput}
              onChange={(event) => setSearchInput(event.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          <Button type="submit" variant="secondary">搜索</Button>
        </form>
        <div>
          <label className="text-xs font-bold" htmlFor="failure-resolved">处理状态</label>
          <select
            id="failure-resolved"
            className="visual-native-select mt-2 min-w-32"
            value={resolved === null ? "" : String(resolved)}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setOffset(0)
              setResolved(event.target.value === "" ? null : event.target.value === "true")
            }}
          >
            <option value="">全部</option>
            <option value="false">待处理</option>
            <option value="true">已给结论</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="ml-auto text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">共 {total} 条</div>
      </div>

      {failures.isError && (
        <p role="alert" className="border border-red-200 bg-danger-wash px-5 py-4 text-sm text-danger rounded-md">
          读取失败：{(failures.error as Error).message}
        </p>
      )}

      <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-[var(--line)] bg-white rounded-md">
        <table className="w-full min-w-[1100px] text-sm">
          <thead className="border-b border-[var(--line-strong)] text-left text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
            <tr>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 font-bold">素材</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 font-bold">上游</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 font-bold">卡在哪</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 font-bold">时间</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 font-bold">结论</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 font-bold">操作</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {rows.length === 0 && !failures.isLoading && (
              <tr><td colSpan={6} className="px-4 py-10 text-center text-[var(--muted)]">当前没有失败素材</td></tr>
            )}
            {rows.map((row) => (
              <FailureTableRow
                key={row.production_run_id}
                row={row}
                onPreview={setPreview}
                expanded={openRun === row.production_run_id}
                onToggle={() => setOpenRun(openRun === row.production_run_id ? null : row.production_run_id)}
                onDone={() => { setOpenRun(null); void refresh() }}
              />
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <Pager
        page={failures.data}
        fallbackLimit={PAGE_SIZE}
        onChange={(next) => setOffset(next)}
        loading={failures.isFetching}
        unit="条素材"
      />

      <p className="px-5 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">
        素材已处理成功但下游未收到？<Link className="ml-2 font-bold underline" to="/workflow/operations#dead-letters">到运行中心处理投递与调度异常</Link>
      </p>
      <InvocationReviewPanel key={categoryKey} categoryKey={categoryKey} />
      <ImageLightbox preview={preview} onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setPreview(null) }} />
    </div>
  )
}

function FailureTableRow({ row, expanded, onToggle, onDone, onPreview }: {
  row: FailureRow
  expanded: boolean
  onToggle: () => void
  onDone: () => void
  onPreview: (preview: ImagePreview) => void
}) {
  const access = useAccess()
  const canWrite = access.can("production:rerun", row.category_key)

  return (
    <>
      <tr className="border-b border-[var(--line)] align-top">
        <td className="px-4 py-3">
          <AssetThumbnail assetId={row.asset_id} alt={`run ${row.production_run_id} ${row.asset_name}`} size="size-20" onPreview={onPreview} />
          <div className="visual-code">run {row.production_run_id}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{categoryLabels[row.category_key] ?? row.category_key}</div>
          {row.asset_name && <div className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{row.asset_name}</div>}
        </td>
        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs font-data">
          <div>task {row.upstream.task_id ?? "—"}</div>
          <div className="text-[var(--muted)]">img {row.upstream.img_id ?? "—"} · ll {row.upstream.ll_id ?? "—"}</div>
          <div className="text-[var(--muted)]">{row.upstream.class_label}</div>
        </td>
        <td className="px-4 py-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Badge tone={row.status === "failed" ? "danger" : "warning"}>{row.status}</Badge>
            <span className="visual-code text-xs">{row.failed_step || "—"}</span>
          </div>
          {row.error_code && <div className="mt-1 text-xs visual-code text-danger">{row.error_code}</div>}
          {row.error_message && (
            <div className="mt-1 max-w-sm text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{row.error_message}</div>
          )}
          <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
            调度尝试 {row.attempt_count} 次（不等于模型调用次数）{row.stage_a_available ? " · 调用 A 有结果" : " · 调用 A 无结果"}
          </div>
        </td>
        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          <div>{formatPlatformTime(row.created_at, "")}</div>
        </td>
        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">
          {row.reassessment ? (
            <div>由 run {row.reassessment.production_run_id} 重评：{row.reassessment.delivered ? "已完成并投递" : row.reassessment.status}</div>
          ) : row.superseded_by ? (
            <Badge tone="success">已由 run {row.superseded_by.production_run_id} 补上</Badge>
          ) : row.resolution ? (
            <>
              <Badge tone="success">{row.resolution.level}{row.category_key === "inspiration_image" && row.resolution.level === "L5" ? " 不通过" : ""} · {row.resolution.score} 分</Badge>
              <div className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">{row.resolution.actor}</div>
              <div className="text-[var(--muted)]">{row.resolution.reason}</div>
            </>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-[var(--muted)]">待处理</span>
          )}
        </td>
        <td className="px-4 py-3">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {!row.reassessment && !row.superseded_by && (
              <Button size="sm" disabled={!canWrite} onClick={onToggle}>{expanded ? "收起" : row.resolution ? "改结论" : "给结论"}</Button>
            )}
            {!row.resolution && !row.reassessment && !row.superseded_by && <RecoveryControl runId={row.production_run_id} categoryKey={row.category_key} onDone={onDone} />}
            {!canWrite && <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">当前角色或类目仅可查看</span>}
          </div>
        </td>
      </tr>
      {expanded && canWrite && (
        <tr className="border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--surface-sunken)]">
          <td colSpan={6} className="px-4 py-5">
            <ResolutionForm row={row} onDone={onDone} />
          </td>
        </tr>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

function ResolutionForm({ row, onDone }: { row: FailureRow; onDone: () => void }) {
  const form = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["failure-resolution-form", row.production_run_id],
    queryFn: () => failureApi.form(row.production_run_id),
  })
  const [level, setLevel] = useState("")
  const [score, setScore] = useState("")
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const [fields, setFields] = useState<ResolutionFields | null>(null)
  const [tagsText, setTagsText] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const inspirationSubmission = useRef<{ fingerprint: string; key: string } | null>(null)

  const prefill = form.data?.prefill
  const inspiration = form.data?.field_profile === "inspiration_image"
  const fieldLabels = inspiration ? INSPIRATION_FIELD_LABELS : FIELD_LABELS
  const effective = useMemo<ResolutionFields>(() => fields ?? {
    title: String(prefill?.title ?? ""),
    ...(inspiration ? {
      seo_title: String(prefill?.seo_title ?? ""),
      class_text: String(prefill?.class_text ?? ""),
      reason: String(prefill?.reason ?? ""),
      trait: String(prefill?.trait ?? ""),
      image_defects: String(prefill?.image_defects ?? ""),
    } : {
      seotitle: String(prefill?.seotitle ?? ""),
      category: String(prefill?.category ?? ""),
    }),
    style: String(prefill?.style ?? ""),
    cons: String(prefill?.cons ?? ""),
    design: String(prefill?.design ?? ""),
    tags: Array.isArray(prefill?.tags) ? (prefill?.tags as string[]) : [],
  }, [fields, prefill, inspiration])

  const tags = useMemo(
    () => ((inspiration ? tagsText !== null : Boolean(tagsText)) ? (tagsText ?? "").split(/[,，\s]+/).filter(Boolean) : effective.tags),
    [tagsText, effective.tags, inspiration],
  )

  const submit = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => {
      const body = { level, score: Number(score), fields: { ...effective, tags }, reason: reason.trim() }
      let key = `failure:${row.production_run_id}:${Date.now()}`
      if (inspiration) {
        // A lost API response must reuse the same submission, not create a new revision.
        const fingerprint = JSON.stringify({ runId: row.production_run_id, ...body })
        if (inspirationSubmission.current?.fingerprint !== fingerprint) {
          inspirationSubmission.current = { fingerprint, key: `failure:${row.production_run_id}:${createIdempotencyToken()}` }
        }
        key = inspirationSubmission.current.key
      }
      return failureApi.resolve(row.production_run_id, { ...body, idempotency_key: key })
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      inspirationSubmission.current = null
      toast.success(inspiration ? "已记下结论并加入下游投递队列" : "已记下结论并投出一版下游")
      onDone()
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => toast.error(`提交失败：${error.message}`),
  })

  if (form.isLoading) return <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">读取表单…</p>
  if (form.isError) return <p className="text-sm text-danger">表单读取失败：{(form.error as Error).message}</p>

  const limits = form.data?.limits ?? {}
  const minTags = form.data?.min_tags ?? 4
  const levels = form.data?.submittable_levels ?? []
  // 逐条列出还差什么，按钮灰着时直接告诉人卡在哪（09-29 铁柱反馈：标题超长但页面看不出来）
  const blockers: string[] = []
  if (!level) blockers.push("未选等级")
  if (score === "") blockers.push("未填分数")
  else if (Number(score) > 100) blockers.push("分数超过 100")
  if (tags.length < minTags) blockers.push(`标签只有 ${tags.length} 个，至少 ${minTags} 个`)
  for (const [key, label] of fieldLabels) {
    const value = String(effective[key] ?? "")
    if ((!inspiration || form.data?.required_fields?.includes(key)) && !value.trim()) blockers.push(`${label}未填`)
    else if (limits[key] && value.length > limits[key]) blockers.push(`${label} ${value.length} 字，超出上限 ${limits[key]} 字`)
  }
  if (!reason.trim()) blockers.push("未填理由")
  const ready = blockers.length === 0

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <p className="max-w-3xl text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
        {inspiration && form.data?.draft_source === "rejected_model_draft"
          ? `模型草稿未通过校验（调用 #${form.data.candidate_invocation_id}）。请人工核对。校验原因：${form.data.validation_reasons?.join("；") || form.data.validation_error}`
          : form.data?.candidate_invocation_id
          ? `已预填调用 #${form.data.candidate_invocation_id} 未通过校验的候选字段。请核对并修复，不代表模型成功结果。${form.data.validation_error ?? ""}`
          : form.data?.stage_a_available
          ? "调用 A 跑成过，字段已按它预填，确认或改完再提交。"
          : inspiration ? "调用 A 没有可用草稿，请人工核对并填写。文本和标签可留空；下游标签为空时按分类、风格生成。"
          : "调用 A 没有产出，下面这些字段要人工填全——下游提交体十三个字段缺一个都投不出去。"}
        {" "}等级只列出下游能打分的档位；分数 0–100。
      </p>
      <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-3">
        <label className="text-xs font-bold">
          等级
          <select
            className="visual-native-select mt-2 w-full"
            value={level}
            onChange={(event) => setLevel(event.target.value)}
          >
            <option value="">请选择</option>
            {levels.map((item) => <option key={item} value={item}>{item}{inspiration && item === "L5" ? " 不通过" : ""}</option>)}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label className="text-xs font-bold">
          分数
          <Input
            className="mt-2 h-11"
            inputMode="numeric"
            value={score}
            onChange={(event) => setScore(event.target.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""))}
            placeholder="0-100"
          />
        </label>
        <label className="text-xs font-bold">
          标签（逗号或空格分隔，{minTags ? `至少 ${minTags} 个` : "可留空"}）
          <Input
            className="mt-2 h-11"
            value={inspiration ? (tagsText ?? effective.tags.join("，")) : (tagsText || effective.tags.join("，"))}
            onChange={(event) => setTagsText(event.target.value)}
          />
          <span className="mt-1 block font-normal text-[var(--muted)]">现在 {tags.length} 个</span>
        </label>
        {fieldLabels.map(([key, label]) => (
          <label key={key} className="text-xs font-bold">
            {label}{limits[key] ? `（≤${limits[key]} 字）` : ""}
            <Input
              className={`mt-2 h-11 ${limits[key] && String(effective[key] ?? "").length > limits[key] ? "!border-danger" : ""}`}
              aria-invalid={Boolean(limits[key] && String(effective[key] ?? "").length > limits[key])}
              value={String(effective[key] ?? "")}
              onChange={(event) => setFields({ ...effective, [key]: event.target.value })}
            />
            {limits[key] ? (
              <span className={`mt-1 block font-normal ${String(effective[key] ?? "").length > limits[key] ? "text-danger" : "text-[var(--muted)]"}`}>
                现在 {String(effective[key] ?? "").length} 字
                {String(effective[key] ?? "").length > limits[key] ? `，超出 ${String(effective[key] ?? "").length - limits[key]} 字` : ""}
              </span>
            ) : null}
          </label>
        ))}
      </div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold">
        {inspiration ? "人工结论理由（必填，记录审核依据）" : "理由（会写进审计与下游覆盖来源）"}
        <Textarea
          className="mt-2"
          rows={2}
          value={reason}
          onChange={(event) => setReason(event.target.value)}
          placeholder="例如：调用 A 反复超时，人工看图后定级"
        />
      </label>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <Button disabled={!ready || submit.isPending} onClick={() => submit.mutate()}>
          提交结论并投下游
        </Button>
        {!ready && <span className="text-xs text-danger">还不能提交：{blockers.join("；")}</span>}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


function RecoveryControl({ runId, categoryKey, onDone }: { runId: number; categoryKey: string; onDone: () => void }) {
  const access = useAccess()
  const canWrite = access.can("production:rerun", categoryKey)
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [requests, setRequests] = useState(1)
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const [confirmed, setConfirmed] = useState(false)
  const preview = useQuery({ queryKey: ["failure-recovery", runId, requests], enabled: open,
    queryFn: () => failureApi.recoveryPreview(runId, requests) })
  const recovery = useMutation({ mutationFn: () => failureApi.recover(runId, {
    quote_hash: preview.data!.quote_hash, requests, reason, confirmed,
  }), onSuccess: () => { toast.success("已按确认额度排队续跑，成功步骤保留"); setOpen(false); onDone() },
    onError: error => { toast.error(error.message); setConfirmed(false); void preview.refetch() } })
  return <div><Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => { setOpen(!open); setConfirmed(false) }}>核验与限次续跑</Button>
    {open && <div className="mt-3 min-w-72 max-w-xl space-y-3 rounded-md border border-[var(--line)] bg-white p-4 text-xs">
      {preview.isPending && <p>读取调用账本与预算…</p>}
      {preview.isError && <p role="alert">读取失败：{preview.error.message}</p>}
      {preview.data && <><p className="font-bold">{preview.data.classification.label} · {preview.data.failed_step}</p>
        <p>{preview.data.classification.advice}</p>
        <p>调度 {preview.data.scheduler_attempts} 次 · 调用账本 {preview.data.model_calls} 条 · 计入额度 {preview.data.charged_calls} 条</p>
        <details className="visual-native-details"><summary className="cursor-pointer">最近调用与请求号</summary>{preview.data.calls.map(call => <p className="mt-2 break-all" key={call.id}>#{call.id} {call.step} · {call.status} · {call.error_code || "无错误"}<br />请求号：{call.request_id ?? "供应商未提供"}</p>)}</details>
        <label className="block">本次最多新增调用 <select className="visual-native-select w-auto max-w-full py-1 pl-2" value={requests} onChange={event => { setRequests(Number(event.target.value)); setConfirmed(false) }}><option value={1}>1 次</option><option value={2}>2 次</option></select></label>
        <p>新增预留费用上限 ¥{(preview.data.max_additional_cost_micros / 1_000_000).toFixed(4)}。既有费用保留；未知请求仍可能计费。达到次数或费用上限即停止，仍可能需要进一步处置。</p>
        {preview.data.blockers.map(message => <p role="alert" key={message}>{message}</p>)}
        <Input aria-label="续跑理由" value={reason} onChange={event => setReason(event.target.value)} placeholder="填写核验结果和续跑理由" maxLength={500} />
        <label className="visual-native-choice min-w-0 flex items-start gap-2"><input className="visual-native-checkbox" type="checkbox" checked={confirmed} onChange={event => setConfirmed(event.target.checked)} />我已核验请求，接受可能重复计费，确认以上次数和费用上限</label>
        <Button size="sm" disabled={!canWrite || !confirmed || !reason.trim() || preview.isFetching || preview.isError || Boolean(preview.data.blockers.length) || recovery.isPending} onClick={() => recovery.mutate()}>确认限次续跑</Button>
      </>}
    </div>}
  </div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/pages/historical-corrections-page.tsx =====
import { useRef, useState } from "react"
import { ArrowLeft, FileXls, ShieldCheck, UploadSimple, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { Link } from "react-router-dom"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"
import type { HistoricalCorrectionPreview } from "@/lib/types"

const roleNames = {
  target_error: "目标错例",
  stable_control: "稳定对照",
  blind_holdout: "锁定盲测集",
  reason_only: "原因专项集",
} as const

export function HistoricalCorrectionsPage() {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([])
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<HistoricalCorrectionPreview | null>(null)
  const upload = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      const body = new FormData()
      files.forEach((file) => body.append("files", file))
      return api<HistoricalCorrectionPreview>("/api/historical-corrections/preview", { method: "POST", body })
    },
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      setPreview(data)
      toast.success(`已只读解析 ${data.summary.unique_item_count} 条高置信人工纠偏记录`)
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        index="07"
        title="历史人工纠偏预览"
        description="只读解析“已处理样本3d&SU”表格，完成字段映射、去重和确定性分层；不会写入素材、Gold或触发模型。"
        actions={<Button asChild variant="secondary"><Link to="/sample-sets"><ArrowLeft />返回样本与回归</Link></Button>}
      />
      <div className="mx-auto shell-content px-page py-7 lg:py-9">
        <section className="grid gap-5 visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white p-5 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto] lg:items-end">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><FileXls size={22} /><h2 className="text-lg font-bold">选择历史纠偏表格</h2></div>
            <p className="mt-2 max-w-[80ch] text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">支持同时选择多份 XLSX。预览保留来源文件与行号；缺少当前维度评分或规则版本的记录不会被伪装成可比较 Gold。</p>
            <input ref={inputRef} type="file" accept=".xlsx" multiple className="sr-only" onChange={(event) => { setFiles(Array.from(event.target.files ?? [])); setPreview(null) }} />
            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()}><UploadSimple />选择 XLSX</Button>
              <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{files.length ? `已选择 ${files.length} 份：${files.map((file) => file.name).join("、")}` : "尚未选择文件"}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <Button onClick={() => upload.mutate()} disabled={!files.length || upload.isPending}>{upload.isPending ? "正在安全解析" : "生成只读预览"}<ShieldCheck /></Button>
        </section>

        <div className="mt-4 flex items-start gap-2 border border-alias-edge-055 bg-alias-surface-079 p-4 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033 rounded-md">
          <WarningCircle className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          此入口当前只负责预览与分层，不会自动导入。正式入库前仍需人工抽检映射、确认资产键并批准Gold候选。
        </div>

        {preview && <>
          <section className="visual-panel-group mt-7 grid grid-cols-2 gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-[var(--line)] md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-6">
            <Metric value={preview.summary.unique_item_count + preview.summary.duplicate_count} label="读取记录" />
            <Metric value={preview.summary.unique_item_count} label="去重后记录" />
            <Metric value={preview.summary.role_counts.target_error ?? 0} label="目标错例" />
            <Metric value={preview.summary.role_counts.stable_control ?? 0} label="稳定对照" />
            <Metric value={preview.summary.role_counts.blind_holdout ?? 0} label="锁定盲测" />
            <Metric value={preview.summary.role_counts.reason_only ?? 0} label="原因专项" />
          </section>

          <section className="mt-7 visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-4">
              <div><h2 className="text-lg font-bold">分层预览</h2><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">当前展示前 {Math.min(200, preview.items.length)} 条，分层由稳定资产键决定，避免重复上传改变盲测集合。</p></div>
              <Badge>{preview.files.length} 份来源文件</Badge>
            </div>
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full min-w-[1120px] border-collapse text-left text-sm">
                <thead><tr className="border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]"><th className="px-4 py-3">来源</th><th className="px-3 py-3">去重键</th><th className="px-3 py-3">模型等级</th><th className="px-3 py-3">人工等级</th><th className="px-3 py-3">人工原因</th><th className="px-4 py-3">样本角色</th></tr></thead>
                <tbody>{preview.items.slice(0, 200).map((item) => <tr key={item.provenance.source_row_sha256} className="border-b border-[var(--line)] last:border-0"><td className="px-4 py-3"><p className="max-w-52 truncate font-semibold" title={item.provenance.source_file}>{item.provenance.source_file}</p><p className="font-data mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{item.provenance.sheet} · 第 {item.provenance.source_row} 行</p></td><td className="font-data max-w-56 truncate px-3 py-3 text-xs" title={item.dedupe_key}>{item.dedupe_key}</td><td className="font-data px-3 py-3 text-lg">{String(item.correction_candidate.model_level || "—")}</td><td className="font-data px-3 py-3 text-lg font-bold">{String(item.correction_candidate.human_level || "—")}</td><td className="max-w-96 px-3 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{item.correction_candidate.reason || "—"}</td><td className="px-4 py-3"><Badge tone={item.sample_role === "blind_holdout" ? "warning" : item.sample_role === "stable_control" ? "success" : "active"}>{roleNames[item.sample_role]}</Badge></td></tr>)}</tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </section>
        </>}
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

function Metric({ value, label }: { value: number; label: string }) {
  return <div className="bg-white p-5"><p className="font-data text-2xl font-semibold">{value}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p></div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/pages/incremental-correction-page.tsx =====
import { useLayoutEffect, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { ArrowClockwise, CaretDown, Funnel, X } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { useAccess } from "@/lib/access"
import { useRememberedPage } from "@/lib/preferences"

import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell"
import { ImageLightbox, type ImagePreview } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Pager } from "@/components/ui/pager"
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { SegmentedControl } from "@/components/ui/segmented-control"
import { BatchDock } from "@/features/incremental-correction/batch-dock"
import { incrementalCorrectionApi } from "@/features/incremental-correction/api"
import { acceptIncrementalCorrections } from "@/features/incremental-correction/correction-cache"
import { CorrectionDashboardPanel } from "@/features/incremental-correction/dashboard-panel"
import { CorrectionDetailDrawer } from "@/features/incremental-correction/detail-drawer"
import { batchLevelOutcomeMessage } from "@/features/incremental-correction/batch-outcome"
import { correctionDeliveryLabel } from "@/features/incremental-correction/delivery-status"
import { MaterialCard, QUICK_CORRECTION_REASON } from "@/features/incremental-correction/material-card"
import {
  abandonSubmission,
  attachSubmissionPage,
  enterSubmission,
  releaseSettledSubmission,
  settleSubmission,
  submissionState,
  subscribeSubmission,
  type SubmissionTicket,
} from "@/features/incremental-correction/submission-gate"
import type {
  CorrectionState,
  IncrementalCorrectionRow,
} from "@/features/incremental-correction/types"

const PAGE_SIZE = 50

const categoryLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  knowledge_graph: "知识图谱",
  inspiration_image: "灵感图",
  model_3d_su: "3D & SU",
  space_image: "空间图片",
  material_image: "材质图",
  design_case: "设计案例",
}

const categoryLabel = (key: string) => categoryLabels[key] ?? key

// 同步入口挡住的这一下没有提交。说清楚，免得以为这张也改好了；连点只保留一条提示。
const notifyStillSaving = () => toast.info("仍在保存上一条，这次没有提交", { id: "incremental-correction-still-saving" })

/** 增量纠偏：100% 队列，先发后审。
 *
 *  这里不是抽查。每条增量素材都在列表里，缩略图和等级直接外显，运营挑有问题的改，
 *  没改的就保持 AI 结果——「没纠偏」只表示运营没提出异议，不代表人工通过，也从不拦住
 *  自动链路。改等级可以直接在列表改，改具体内容进详情改，两者都会覆盖 AI 结果写下游。 */
export function IncrementalCorrectionPage() {
  const access = useAccess()
  const remembered = useRememberedPage("incremental-corrections")
  const pageSize = [25, 50, 100].includes(remembered.table.page_size ?? 0) ? remembered.table.page_size! : PAGE_SIZE
  const client = useQueryClient()
  // Mutation pending is rendered later. Own the list's write entrance now,
  // until the response and authoritative local cache have been accepted.
  // The entrance lives on the QueryClient, so leaving the route and coming
  // back while a save is still pending keeps it held (see submission-gate).
  // Settling is a state change of its own, so a page that came back mid-save
  // re-renders and hands the entrance back below without waiting for a read.
  const submission = useSyncExternalStore(
    (listener) => subscribeSubmission(client, listener),
    () => submissionState(client),
  )
  const submitting = submission !== "idle"
  useLayoutEffect(() => attachSubmissionPage(client), [client])
  const [categoryKey, setCategoryKey] = remembered.field<string>("category_key", "")
  const [workflowVersionId, setWorkflowVersionId] = remembered.field<number | null>("workflow_version_id", null)
  const [state, setState] = remembered.field<CorrectionState | null>("state", null)
  const [level, setLevel] = remembered.field<string | null>("level", null)
  const [upstreamClassId, setUpstreamClassId] = remembered.field<number | null>("upstream_class_id", null)
  const [correctedBy, setCorrectedBy] = remembered.field<string>("corrected_by", "")
  const [publishedFrom, setPublishedFrom] = remembered.field<string>("published_from", "")
  const [publishedTo, setPublishedTo] = remembered.field<string>("published_to", "")
  const [searchDraft, setSearchDraft] = useState("")
  const [search, setSearch] = remembered.field<string>("search", "")
  const [offset, setOffset] = useState(0)
  // 勾选只在当前页内有意义：翻页、换筛选都清掉，避免把看不见的素材一起改了。
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<Set<number>>(() => new Set())
  const [batchLevel, setBatchLevel] = useState("L2")
  const [batchReason, setBatchReason] = useState("L1 准确率低，先全部回捞到 L2")
  const [detailId, setDetailId] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<ImagePreview | null>(null)

  const [moreFiltersOpen, setMoreFiltersOpen] = useState(false)

  const queryKey = ["incremental-corrections", categoryKey, workflowVersionId, state, level,
    upstreamClassId, correctedBy, publishedFrom, publishedTo, search, offset, pageSize]
  const queue = useQuery({
    queryKey,
    queryFn: () => incrementalCorrectionApi.list({
      categoryKey: categoryKey || undefined,
      workflowVersionId,
      state,
      level,
      upstreamClassId,
      correctedBy: correctedBy || null,
      publishedFrom: publishedFrom || null,
      publishedTo: publishedTo || null,
      search: search || null,
      limit: pageSize,
      offset,
    }),
    placeholderData: (previous) => previous,
    refetchInterval: 15_000,
  })
  const filters = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["incremental-correction-filters"],
    queryFn: incrementalCorrectionApi.filters,
  })
  // 看板跟着类目/工作流走，但不跟着纠偏状态和等级走——按「未纠偏」筛完再看
  // 纠偏率，分母就成了「未纠偏的里有多少被纠偏」，永远是 0。
  const dashboard = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["incremental-correction-dashboard", categoryKey, workflowVersionId],
    queryFn: () => incrementalCorrectionApi.dashboard({
      categoryKey: categoryKey || undefined,
      workflowVersionId,
      window: 1000,
    }),
    refetchInterval: 60_000,
  })

  const correct = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ row, level: target, reason, idempotencyKey }: {
      row: IncrementalCorrectionRow
      level: string
      reason: string
      idempotencyKey: string
      ticket: SubmissionTicket
    }) => incrementalCorrectionApi.correctLevel(row.evaluation_id, {
      level: target,
      reason,
      idempotency_key: idempotencyKey,
      expected_level: row.level,
    }),
    onSuccess: async (row) => {
      await acceptIncrementalCorrections(client, [row])
      const notify = ["rejected", "dead_lettered"].includes(row.downstream.delivery_status ?? "")
        ? toast.warning : toast.success
      notify(`已保存为 ${row.level}；${correctionDeliveryLabel(row.downstream)}`)
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => {
      // 冲突多半是别人刚改过；先把最新内容拉回来，运营才看得到自己在跟谁抢。
      void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["incremental-corrections"] })
      toast.error(error.message)
    },
    onSettled: (_row, _error, input) => settleSubmission(input.ticket),
  })

  const batch = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: { rows: IncrementalCorrectionRow[]; level: string; reason: string; idempotencyKey: string; ticket: SubmissionTicket }) =>
      incrementalCorrectionApi.batchCorrectLevel({
        items: input.rows.map((row) => ({ evaluation_id: row.evaluation_id, expected_level: row.level })),
        level: input.level,
        reason: input.reason,
        idempotency_key: input.idempotencyKey,
      }),
    onSuccess: async (outcome, input) => {
      setSelectedIds(new Set())
      await acceptIncrementalCorrections(client, outcome.corrected)
      ;(outcome.skipped.length ? toast.warning : toast.success)(batchLevelOutcomeMessage(outcome, input.level))
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => {
      void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["incremental-corrections"] })
      toast.error(error.message)
    },
    onSettled: (_outcome, _error, input) => settleSubmission(input.ticket),
  })

  // Hand the entrance back after the completed write/cache state is committed
  // to the controls too. This also covers a response faster than pending's
  // first render; there is no delay or wait for aggregate queries.
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!correct.isPending && !batch.isPending) releaseSettledSubmission(client)
  })

  async function submitCorrection(input: { row: IncrementalCorrectionRow; level: string; reason: string }): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!access.can("reviews:write", input.row.category_key)) { toast.info(access.readOnlyReason); return false }
    const ticket = enterSubmission(client)
    if (!ticket) { notifyStillSaving(); return false }
    try {
      await correct.mutateAsync({ ...input, ticket, idempotencyKey: `${input.row.evaluation_id}:${input.level}:${Date.now()}` })
      return true
    } catch {
      // 网络失败由 onError 提示、onSettled 结清，仍等完成状态渲染后交还入口。
      // 只有请求尚未进入 mutation 时的异常才直接撤回。
      if (!ticket.settled) abandonSubmission(ticket)
      return false
    }
  }

  function submitBatch(input: { rows: IncrementalCorrectionRow[]; level: string; reason: string }) {
    if (input.rows.some((row) => !access.can("reviews:write", row.category_key))) { toast.info(access.readOnlyReason); return }
    const ticket = enterSubmission(client)
    if (!ticket) { notifyStillSaving(); return }
    try {
      batch.mutate({ ...input, ticket, idempotencyKey: `batch:${input.level}:${Date.now()}` })
    } catch (error) {
      abandonSubmission(ticket)
      throw error
    }
  }

  async function refresh() {
    await Promise.all([
      client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["incremental-corrections"] }),
      client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["incremental-correction-filters"] }),
      client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["incremental-correction-dashboard"] }),
    ])
  }

  function applyFilter(change: () => void) {
    change()
    setOffset(0)
    setSelectedIds(new Set())
  }

  const rows = queue.data?.items ?? []
  const writableRows = rows.filter((row) => access.can("reviews:write", row.category_key))
  const selectedRows = rows.filter((row) => selectedIds.has(row.evaluation_id))
  const allOnPageSelected = writableRows.length > 0 && writableRows.every((row) => selectedIds.has(row.evaluation_id))
  // 批量只对能改成目标等级的素材有意义；本页各素材可选等级的交集就是批量能选的等级。
  // 暂不开放的目标等级（灵感图跨 L5）也排除；整条被锁的素材（如灵感图当前 L5）由后端逐条跳过并说明原因。
  const fullyLocked = (row: (typeof rows)[number]) =>
    row.selectable_levels.every((item) => (row.locked_levels ?? []).includes(item))
  const batchLevels = ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"].filter((item) =>
    rows.length > 0 && rows.every((row) => row.selectable_levels.includes(item))
    && rows.every((row) => fullyLocked(row) || !(row.locked_levels ?? []).includes(item)))

  function toggleAllOnPage(checked: boolean) {
    setSelectedIds(checked ? new Set(writableRows.map((row) => row.evaluation_id)) : new Set())
  }
  function toggleOne(id: number, checked: boolean) {
    setSelectedIds((previous) => {
      const next = new Set(previous)
      if (checked) next.add(id)
      else next.delete(id)
      return next
    })
  }
  const workflowOptions = (filters.data?.workflows ?? []).filter(
    (item) => !categoryKey || item.category_key === categoryKey,
  )

  const moreFilterCount = [categoryKey, workflowVersionId, upstreamClassId, correctedBy, publishedFrom, publishedTo].filter(Boolean).length
