  const activeFilters = [
    { label: "纠偏状态", value: state ? (filters.data?.states ?? []).find((item) => item.key === state)?.label ?? state : "", clear: () => setState(null) },
    { label: "等级", value: level, clear: () => setLevel(null) },
    { label: "ID 搜索", value: search, clear: () => { setSearchDraft(""); setSearch("") } },
    { label: "类目", value: categoryKey ? categoryLabel(categoryKey) : "", clear: () => { setCategoryKey(""); setWorkflowVersionId(null) } },
    { label: "工作流版本", value: workflowVersionId ? workflowOptions.find((item) => item.workflow_version_id === workflowVersionId)?.version ?? `#${workflowVersionId}` : "", clear: () => setWorkflowVersionId(null) },
    { label: "上游分类", value: upstreamClassId ? (filters.data?.upstream_classes ?? []).find((item) => item.class_id === upstreamClassId)?.label ?? upstreamClassId : "", clear: () => setUpstreamClassId(null) },
    { label: "操作人", value: correctedBy, clear: () => setCorrectedBy("") },
    { label: "发布时间（起）", value: publishedFrom, clear: () => setPublishedFrom("") },
    { label: "发布时间（止）", value: publishedTo, clear: () => setPublishedTo("") },
  ].filter((item) => Boolean(item.value))

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        index="02.5"
        title="增量纠偏"
        description="所有增量素材都在这里，不抽样也不阻断。人工修改自动保存并提交下游；是否接收以每条素材的投递状态为准，没改的保持 AI 结果。"
        actions={(
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => refresh()} disabled={queue.isFetching}>
            <ArrowClockwise />刷新
          </Button>
        )}
      />
      <div className="mx-auto shell-content min-w-0 space-y-6 px-5 py-7 md:px-8 lg:py-9">
        <CorrectionDashboardPanel data={dashboard.data} loading={dashboard.isLoading} />

        <Card data-testid="correction-filters" className="space-y-4" narrow>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-4">
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground">纠偏状态</p>
              <SegmentedControl label="纠偏状态" value={state ?? ""}
                options={[{ value: "", label: "全部" }, ...(filters.data?.states ?? []).map((item) => ({ value: item.key, label: item.label }))]}
                onValueChange={(value) => applyFilter(() => setState((value || null) as CorrectionState | null))} />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground">等级</p>
              <SegmentedControl label="等级" value={level ?? ""}
                options={[{ value: "", label: "全部等级" }, ...["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"].map((item) => ({ value: item, label: item }))]}
                onValueChange={(value) => applyFilter(() => setLevel(value || null))} />
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-[1_1_20rem]">
          <Filter label="ID 搜索">
            <form
              className="flex gap-1"
              onSubmit={(event) => { event.preventDefault(); applyFilter(() => setSearch(searchDraft.trim())) }}
            >
              <Input
                className="min-w-0 flex-1"
                placeholder="评测 / 素材 / task / img / ll id"
                value={searchDraft}
                onChange={(event) => setSearchDraft(event.target.value)}
              />
              <Button type="submit" size="sm" variant="secondary" className="h-11">找</Button>
              {search && (
                <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="ghost" className="h-11" onClick={() => { setSearchDraft(""); applyFilter(() => setSearch("")) }}>清</Button>
              )}
            </form>
          </Filter>
            </div>
            <Button variant="secondary" aria-label="更多筛选" aria-expanded={moreFiltersOpen} aria-controls="incremental-more-filters"
              onClick={() => setMoreFiltersOpen((open) => !open)}>
              <Funnel />更多筛选{moreFilterCount > 0 && <span className="font-data">（{moreFilterCount}）</span>}<CaretDown />
            </Button>
          </div>
          <div id="incremental-more-filters" hidden={!moreFiltersOpen}>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 border-t border-line pt-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
          <Filter label="类目">
            <select
              className="visual-native-select w-full min-w-0"
              value={categoryKey}
              onChange={(event) => applyFilter(() => {
                setCategoryKey(event.target.value)
                setWorkflowVersionId(null)
              })}
            >
              <option value="">全部类目</option>
              {(filters.data?.categories ?? []).map((item) => (
                <option key={item.category_key} value={item.category_key}>
                  {categoryLabel(item.category_key)}（{item.total}）
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </Filter>
          <Filter label="工作流版本">
            <select
              className="visual-native-select w-full min-w-0"
              value={workflowVersionId ?? ""}
              onChange={(event) => applyFilter(() => setWorkflowVersionId(
                event.target.value ? Number(event.target.value) : null,
              ))}
            >
              <option value="">全部版本</option>
              {workflowOptions.map((item) => (
                <option key={item.workflow_version_id} value={item.workflow_version_id}>
                  {item.version ?? `#${item.workflow_version_id}`}（{item.total}）
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </Filter>
          <Filter label="上游分类">
            <select
              className="visual-native-select w-full min-w-0"
              value={upstreamClassId ?? ""}
              onChange={(event) => applyFilter(() => setUpstreamClassId(
                event.target.value ? Number(event.target.value) : null,
              ))}
            >
              <option value="">全部分类</option>
              {(filters.data?.upstream_classes ?? []).map((item) => (
                <option key={item.class_id} value={item.class_id}>
                  {item.label}（{item.total}）
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </Filter>
          <Filter label="操作人">
            <select
              className="visual-native-select w-full min-w-0"
              value={correctedBy}
              onChange={(event) => applyFilter(() => setCorrectedBy(event.target.value))}
            >
              <option value="">不限</option>
              {(filters.data?.correctors ?? []).map((item) => (
                <option key={item.corrector} value={item.corrector}>
                  {item.corrector}（{item.total}）
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </Filter>
          <Filter label="发布时间（起）">
            <Input
              type="date"
              className="min-w-0 w-full"
              value={publishedFrom}
              max={publishedTo || undefined}
              onChange={(event) => applyFilter(() => setPublishedFrom(event.target.value))}
            />
          </Filter>
          <Filter label="发布时间（止）">
            <Input
              type="date"
              className="min-w-0 w-full"
              value={publishedTo}
              min={publishedFrom || undefined}
              onChange={(event) => applyFilter(() => setPublishedTo(event.target.value))}
            />
          </Filter>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
              <p className="min-w-0 flex-[1_1_20rem] text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
                多人同时纠偏时，各自筛一段来处理即可；有人先改了同一条，这里会提示刷新，不会把对方的结论覆盖掉。
              </p>
              <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => applyFilter(() => {
                setCategoryKey(""); setWorkflowVersionId(null); setUpstreamClassId(null)
                setCorrectedBy(""); setPublishedFrom(""); setPublishedTo("")
              })}>清除这几项</Button>
            </div>
          </div>
          {(state || level || search || moreFilterCount > 0) && (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-t border-line pt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground" aria-label="当前生效的筛选">
              <span>当前条件</span>
              {activeFilters.map((item) => <Button key={item.label} size="sm" variant="secondary" className="max-w-full whitespace-normal break-words text-left" aria-label={`清除${item.label}`}
                onClick={() => applyFilter(item.clear)}>{item.label}：{item.value}<X /></Button>)}
            </div>
          )}
        </Card>

        <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
          勾选后被别人先改过的会跳过并提示，不会盖掉对方的结论；单张卡上直接点等级会自动保存并提交下游（理由记为「{QUICK_CORRECTION_REASON}」）。
        </p>

        <section data-testid="correction-queue" className="min-w-0">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 pb-4">
            <label className="visual-native-choice flex min-h-hit items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                className="visual-native-checkbox"
                checked={allOnPageSelected}
                disabled={!writableRows.length}
                onChange={(event) => toggleAllOnPage(event.target.checked)}
              />
              全选本页（{selectedRows.length}/{rows.length}）
            </label>
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
              点图看原图；等级就地改，改完自动保存并提交下游。要改分类、标题这些内容，进「纠偏具体内容」。
            </p>
            <p className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
              {queue.data ? `共 ${queue.data.total} 条` : ""}
            </p>
          </div>
          {rows.length > 0 && (
            /* CSS 多列即瀑布流：卡片高度由图片原始宽高比决定，不裁剪、不统一高度，
               break-inside-avoid 保证一张卡不会被切成两栏。 */
            <div className="columns-1 gap-4 pb-2 sm:columns-2 lg:columns-3 2xl:columns-4">
              {rows.map((row) => (
                <MaterialCard
                  key={row.evaluation_id}
                  row={row}
                  canWrite={access.can("reviews:write", row.category_key)}
                  pending={submitting || correct.isPending || batch.isPending}
                  selected={selectedIds.has(row.evaluation_id)}
                  onSelect={(checked) => toggleOne(row.evaluation_id, checked)}
                  onPreview={setPreview}
                  onDetail={() => setDetailId(row.evaluation_id)}
                  onCorrect={({ level: target, reason }) => submitCorrection({ row, level: target, reason })}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {!rows.length && !queue.isLoading && (
            <div className="px-5 py-12 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">
              {offset > 0 ? "这一页已经没有素材了。" : "当前筛选条件下没有增量素材。"}
              {offset > 0 && (
                <Button className="ml-3" size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => setOffset(0)}>
                  回到第一页
                </Button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
          {queue.isLoading && (
            <div className="px-5 py-12 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">正在读取增量素材…</div>
          )}
          {queue.data && queue.data.total > 0 && (
            <div className="border-t border-[var(--line)]">
              <label className="flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap px-5 pt-3 text-xs font-semibold">每页<select aria-label="每页纠偏素材数量" className="visual-native-select" value={pageSize} disabled={!remembered.ready} onChange={(event) => { remembered.setTable({ page_size: Number(event.target.value) }); setOffset(0); setSelectedIds(new Set()) }}>{[25, 50, 100].map((size) => <option key={size} value={size}>{size} 条</option>)}</select></label>
              <Pager
                showPageNumbers
                page={queue.data}
                fallbackLimit={pageSize}
                onChange={(next) => { setOffset(next); setSelectedIds(new Set()) }}
                loading={queue.isLoading || queue.isPlaceholderData}
                unit="条素材"
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
        {selectedRows.length > 0 && (
          <BatchDock>
            <p className="font-data text-sm font-semibold">已选 {selectedRows.length} 条</p>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1" role="group" aria-label="批量改为">
              {batchLevels.map((item) => (
                <button
                  key={item}
                  type="button"
                  aria-pressed={item === batchLevel}
                  className={`visual-control min-h-hit min-w-hit rounded-md border px-3 py-2 font-data text-xs ${
                    item === batchLevel ? "border-active-border bg-active-surface text-active-text font-bold" : "border-line-strong bg-canvas"
                  }`}
                  onClick={() => setBatchLevel(item)}
                >
                  {item}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <Input
              className="min-w-0 flex-[1_1_16rem]"
              value={batchReason}
              placeholder="批量改等级的理由"
              onChange={(event) => setBatchReason(event.target.value)}
            />
            <Button
              size="sm"
              className="max-w-full whitespace-normal text-left"
              disabled={submitting || correct.isPending || batch.isPending || selectedRows.length === 0 || selectedRows.some((row) => !access.can("reviews:write", row.category_key)) || !batchReason.trim() || !batchLevels.includes(batchLevel)}
              onClick={() => submitBatch({ rows: selectedRows, level: batchLevel, reason: batchReason.trim() })}
            >
              把选中的 {selectedRows.length} 条改为 {batchLevel} 并提交下游
            </Button>
            <p className="basis-full text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
              勾选后被别人先改过的会跳过并提示，不会盖掉对方的结论；单张卡上直接点等级会自动保存并提交下游（理由记为「{QUICK_CORRECTION_REASON}」）。
            </p>
          </BatchDock>
        )}

      </div>

      <CorrectionDetailDrawer
        evaluationId={detailId}
        onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setDetailId(null) }}
        onCorrected={(row) => { void acceptIncrementalCorrections(client, [row]) }}
      />
      <ImageLightbox preview={preview} onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setPreview(null) }} />
    </>
  )
}

function Filter({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">
      <span className="mb-2 block">{label}</span>
      {children}
    </label>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/pages/incremental-dashboard-page.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { Link, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom"
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"
import type { IncrementalDashboard, IncrementalDailyPoint } from "@/lib/types"
import { formatCoverage } from "@/features/incremental-dashboard/coverage"

const display = (v: number | null | undefined) => v == null ? "—" : v.toLocaleString("zh-CN")
const today = () => new Date(Date.now() + 8 * 3600_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10)
const previousDays = (day: string, days: number) => new Date(Date.parse(day + "T00:00:00Z") - days * 86400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10)

export function IncrementalDashboardPage() {
  const [params] = useSearchParams()
  const category = params.get("category_key") ?? ""
  const workflow = Number(params.get("workflow_definition_id"))
  const [start, setStart] = useState(previousDays(today(), 6))
  const [end, setEnd] = useState(today())
  const [comparison, setComparison] = useState("week")
  const [range, setRange] = useState({ start, end, comparison })
  const validScope = Boolean(category) && Number.isInteger(workflow) && workflow > 0
  const data = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["incremental-dashboard", category, workflow, range], enabled: validScope,
    queryFn: () => api<IncrementalDashboard>(`/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/${encodeURIComponent(category)}/${workflow}/dashboard?` + new URLSearchParams(range)),
  })
  const result = data.data
  const summary = result?.summary
  const change = summary?.platform_success != null && summary.comparison_success != null && summary.comparison_success > 0
    ? ((summary.platform_success / summary.comparison_success - 1) * 100).toFixed(1) + "%" : "无可比增幅"
  return <>
    <PageHeader index="02" title={result ? `${result.workflow_name} · 数据看板` : "增量数据看板"}
      description="按北京时间查看每日产出与累计处理量。模型重试不重复计数；下游投递与平台产出分别统计。"
      actions={<Button variant="secondary" asChild><Link to="/workflow/incremental">返回自动化链路</Link></Button>} />
    <div className="mx-auto shell-content min-w-0 px-page space-y-6 py-7">
      {!validScope ? <p role="alert">请从对应工作流卡片的“数据看板”入口进入。</p> : <>
        <form className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-4" onSubmit={e => { e.preventDefault(); setRange({ start, end, comparison }) }}>
          <label className="text-sm">开始日期<input className="visual-native-input w-auto max-w-full ml-2 focus-within:outline-2 focus-within:outline-solid focus-within:outline-ink focus-within:outline-offset-2 focus-within:ring-4 focus-within:ring-brand" type="date" required value={start} max={end} onChange={e => setStart(e.target.value)} /></label>
          <label className="text-sm">结束日期<input className="visual-native-input w-auto max-w-full ml-2 focus-within:outline-2 focus-within:outline-solid focus-within:outline-ink focus-within:outline-offset-2 focus-within:ring-4 focus-within:ring-brand" type="date" required value={end} min={start} max={today()} onChange={e => setEnd(e.target.value)} /></label>
          <label className="text-sm">比较<select className="visual-native-select w-auto max-w-full ml-2" value={comparison} onChange={e => setComparison(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="none">不比较</option><option value="year">去年同期</option><option value="month">上月同期</option><option value="week">上周同期</option><option value="day">前一天</option>
          </select></label><Button disabled={data.isFetching} type="submit">应用日期</Button>
        </form>
        {data.isLoading && <p>正在读取每日数据…</p>}
        {data.isError && <p role="alert" className="text-danger">读取失败：{(data.error as Error).message}</p>}
        {result && <>
          <p className="border border-amber-200 bg-warning-wash px-4 py-3 text-sm rounded-md">上游全量历史尚未接通，处置覆盖率暂不可计算。{result.history_note}</p>
          <section aria-label="期间指标" className="grid gap-px border border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 rounded-md overflow-hidden">
            <Metric label="每日上游增量 · 期间合计" value={display(summary?.upstream_increment)} hint="未接入上游全量，不能用建单量代替" />
            <Metric label="平台成功处理 · 期间合计" value={display(summary?.platform_success)} hint={`比较期间 ${display(summary?.comparison_success)} · ${change}`} />
            <Metric label="处置覆盖率" value={formatCoverage(summary?.coverage)} hint="等待同批上游分母，不补零" />
            <Metric label="累计处理量" value={display(summary?.cumulative_success)} hint={`截至 ${result.end} · 业务素材去重`} />
          </section>
          <section className="rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line)] bg-white p-card"><h2 className="visual-card-title">每日平台成功处理量</h2>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{result.start} 至 {result.end} · 深色实线：当前期间；浅色虚线：比较期间。断线表示无数据。</p>
            <DailyChart points={result.series} previous={result.comparison_series} />
          </section>
          <p className="text-xs leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">{result.scope_note} 本期平台建单量：{display(summary?.platform_admitted)}。可见记录起点：{result.history_start ?? "暂无"}。累计只涵盖平台保留记录。</p>
          <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-[var(--line)] bg-white rounded-md"><table className="w-full text-left text-sm"><thead><tr>{["日期", "上游增量", "平台建单量", "成功处理", "比较日期", "比较期成功", "覆盖率"].map(t => <th className="px-4 py-3" key={t}>{t}</th>)}</tr></thead><tbody>
            {result.series.map((p, i) => <tr className="border-t border-[var(--line)]" key={p.date}><td className="px-4 py-3">{p.date}</td><td>{display(p.upstream_increment)}</td><td>{display(p.platform_admitted)}</td><td>{display(p.platform_success)}</td><td>{result.comparison_series[i]?.date ?? "—"}</td><td>{display(result.comparison_series[i]?.platform_success)}</td><td>{formatCoverage(p.coverage)}</td></tr>)}
          </tbody></table></div>
        </>}
      </>}
    </div>
  </>
}
function Metric({ label, value, hint }: { label: string; value: string; hint: string }) {
  return <div className="bg-white p-5"><p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p><p className="my-3 font-data text-3xl font-bold">{value}</p><p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{hint}</p></div>
}
function DailyChart({ points, previous }: { points: IncrementalDailyPoint[]; previous: IncrementalDailyPoint[] }) {
  const max = Math.max(1, ...[...points, ...previous].map(p => p.platform_success ?? 0))
  const x = (i: number) => 70 + (points.length === 1 ? 425 : 850 * i / (points.length - 1))
  const y = (n: number) => 255 - n / max * 210
  const path = (items: IncrementalDailyPoint[]) => {
    let gap = true
    return items.map((p, i) => { if (p.platform_success == null) { gap = true; return "" }; const v = `${gap ? "M" : "L"}${x(i)},${y(p.platform_success)}`; gap = false; return v }).join(" ")
  }
  return <svg role="img" aria-label="每日成功处理折线图，详细数值见下方表格" viewBox="0 0 960 305" className="mt-5 w-full">
    {[0, .25, .5, .75, 1].map(r => <g key={r}><line x1="70" x2="920" y1={y(r * max)} y2={y(r * max)} stroke="#e1e5db" /><text x="8" y={y(r * max) + 4} fontSize="12" fill="#68705c">{Math.round(max * r)}</text></g>)}
    <path d={path(previous)} fill="none" stroke="#aabf77" strokeWidth="3" strokeDasharray="6 5" />
    <path d={path(points)} fill="none" stroke="#536820" strokeWidth="3" />
    {points.map((p, i) => <g key={p.date}>{p.platform_success != null && <circle cx={x(i)} cy={y(p.platform_success)} r="3" fill="#536820"><title>{p.date}：{p.platform_success}</title></circle>}{(i % Math.max(1, Math.ceil(points.length / 8)) === 0 || i === points.length - 1) && <text x={x(i)} y="284" textAnchor="middle" fontSize="12" fill="#68705c">{p.date?.slice(5)}</text>}</g>)}
  </svg>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/pages/incremental-workspace-page.tsx =====
import { ArrowRight, CheckCircle, Images, Package, Play, Sparkle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMemo, useState } from "react"
import { Link } from "react-router-dom"
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"

import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell"
import { ContentIdentityDrawer } from "@/components/content-identity-drawer"
import { WorkflowContextBadge } from "@/components/workflow-context-badge"
import { WorkflowStepper, type WorkflowStep } from "@/components/workflow-stepper"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { api, contentIngressApi, sourceIdentityApi } from "@/lib/api"
import { evaluationProductionApi } from "@/lib/evaluation-packages"
import { IncrementalAutomationPanel } from "@/features/incremental-automation/automation-panel"
import type { AuditEvent, ContentIdentityRecord, EvaluationCategoryProfile, EvaluationProductionRun, MaterialPackage } from "@/lib/types"

// 2026-09-17：三路轮询各 4 秒，一轮就是 ~1MB（审计事件 360KB、进料记录 300KB、包列表 340KB），
// 首页在人多时读起来比后端慢得多；这些数字只用来画计数和最近 5 条，15 秒足够。
const WORKSPACE_POLL_MS = 15_000

export function IncrementalWorkspacePage() {
  const [manualOpen, setManualOpen] = useState(false)
  const [selectedIdentityId, setSelectedIdentityId] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const categories = useQuery({
    enabled: manualOpen,
    queryKey: ["evaluation-categories", "incremental"],
    queryFn: () => api<{ items: EvaluationCategoryProfile[] }>("/api/evaluation-categories"),
  })
  const packages = useQuery({
    enabled: manualOpen,
    queryKey: ["material-packages", "incremental"],
    queryFn: () => api<{ items: MaterialPackage[] }>("/api/material-packages?limit=500"),
  })
  const runs = useQuery({
    enabled: manualOpen,
    queryKey: ["evaluation-production-runs", "incremental"],
    queryFn: () => evaluationProductionApi.list("incremental"),
    refetchInterval: WORKSPACE_POLL_MS,
  })
  const ingressEvents = useQuery({
    enabled: manualOpen,
    queryKey: ["audit-events", "content-ingress", "incremental"],
    queryFn: () => api<{ items: AuditEvent[] }>("/api/audit-events?category=content_ingress&limit=500"),
    refetchInterval: WORKSPACE_POLL_MS,
  })
  const contentRecords = useQuery({
    enabled: manualOpen,
    queryKey: ["content-ingress-records", "incremental"],
    queryFn: contentIngressApi.list,
    refetchInterval: WORKSPACE_POLL_MS,
  })
  const identityVerifications = useQuery({
    enabled: manualOpen,
    queryKey: ["source-identity-verifications", "incremental"],
    queryFn: sourceIdentityApi.list,
  })
  const activeCategoryCount = useMemo(
    () => (categories.data?.items ?? []).filter((item) => item.status === "active").length,
    [categories.data?.items],
  )
  const availablePackages = packages.data?.items ?? []
  const latestRun = (runs.data?.items ?? [])[0] as EvaluationProductionRun | undefined
  const ingressCounts = useMemo(() => {
    const counts = { received: 0, awaiting: 0, packaged: 0, blocked: 0, duplicate: 0 }
    for (const event of ingressEvents.data?.items ?? []) {
      if (event.action === "incremental_package_created") {
        counts.packaged += 1
      } else if (event.action === "duplicate_reused") {
        counts.duplicate += 1
      } else if (event.action === "awaiting_material") {
        counts.awaiting += 1
      } else if (event.action === "stale" || event.action === "blocked_profile" || event.action === "blocked_identity") {
        counts.blocked += 1
      } else {
        counts.received += 1
      }
    }
    return counts
  }, [ingressEvents.data?.items])
  const recentContentRecords = (contentRecords.data?.items ?? []).slice(0, 5)
  const selectedIdentity = recentContentRecords.find((item) => item.id === selectedIdentityId) ?? null
  const selectedVerification = (identityVerifications.data?.items ?? []).find((item) => item.id === selectedIdentity?.identity_verification_id) ?? null
  const currentStep = latestRun?.status === "published" ? 8 : latestRun?.status === "awaiting_review" ? 6 : latestRun ? 4 : 2
  const steps: WorkflowStep[] = [
    { key: "category", label: "选择类目", note: `${activeCategoryCount} 个类目可用`, state: currentStep > 1 ? "completed" : "current", required: true },
    { key: "ingress", label: "导入增量素材", note: "手动导入或接入合同模拟事件", state: currentStep > 2 ? "completed" : "current", required: true },
    { key: "package", label: "编辑评测包", note: "复杂内容在二级编辑器完成", state: currentStep > 3 ? "completed" : "current", required: true },
    { key: "evaluate", label: "启动评测", note: "固定机制、模型和素材快照", state: currentStep >= 4 ? "current" : "pending", required: true },
    { key: "review", label: "人工纠偏", note: "只处理需要人工判断的结果", state: currentStep >= 6 ? "current" : "pending" },
    { key: "candidate", label: "AI 迭代与回归", note: "候选自动生成，结果等待人工决策", state: currentStep >= 7 ? "current" : "pending" },
    { key: "release", label: "正式发布", note: "机制轴与标签事实轴分开", state: currentStep >= 8 ? "completed" : "pending" },
  ]

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        index="01"
        title="增量自动化"
        description="管理各类目工作流的增量自动化链路，查看运行状态与积压，独立控制自动派发。工作流编排与版本管理继续在工作流管理页完成。"
        context={<WorkflowContextBadge kind="incremental" />}
        actions={<><Button asChild variant="secondary"><Link to="/workflow/incremental/correction"><CheckCircle />增量纠偏</Link></Button><Button asChild variant="secondary"><Link to="/workflow/materials/packages"><Images />导入或整理素材</Link></Button></>}
      />
      <div className="mx-auto shell-content min-w-0 px-page space-y-6 py-7 lg:py-9">
        <IncrementalAutomationPanel />
        <details onToggle={event => setManualOpen(event.currentTarget.open)} className="visual-native-details border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4"><summary className="cursor-pointer text-sm font-bold">手动增量操作与接入记录</summary><div className="mt-5 space-y-6">
        <WorkflowStepper workflowLabel="增量素材 → 评测 → 纠偏 → 候选回归 → 发布" steps={steps.slice(0, 5)} />
        <section className="visual-panel-group grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-[var(--line)] lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.3fr)_minmax(320px,0.7fr)]">
          <div className="bg-white px-5 py-6 md:px-6">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4"><div><p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">主操作</p><h2 className="visual-card-title mt-2">选择类目并进入增量生产</h2></div><Badge tone={availablePackages.length ? "success" : "warning"}>{availablePackages.length ? `${availablePackages.length} 个素材包可用` : "等待素材包"}</Badge></div>
            <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              <div className="border border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken p-4 rounded-md"><div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Sparkle size={18} /><p className="text-sm font-bold">类目路由</p></div><p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">按活动类目 profile 进入对应评测机制；未知 profile 只读诊断，不启动任务。</p><Button asChild size="sm" variant="secondary" className="mt-4"><Link to="/workflow/optimization/category-evaluation-v3-config?workflow_kind=incremental"><Package />查看类目机制</Link></Button></div>
              <div className="border border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken p-4 rounded-md"><div className="flex items-center gap-2"><CheckCircle size={18} /><p className="text-sm font-bold">当前可执行项</p></div><p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">评测启动、人工纠偏、候选回归和发布都沿既有人工闸门执行，不自动采纳。</p><Button asChild size="sm" className="mt-4"><Link to="/workflow/production-line">进入生产线<ArrowRight /></Link></Button></div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <aside className="bg-active-surface px-5 py-6 md:px-6"><p className="text-xs font-bold">最近一次增量运行</p>{latestRun ? <><p className="mt-3 text-lg font-semibold">{latestRun.material_package.name}</p><p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{latestRun.category.name} · {latestRun.current_stage_label} · {latestRun.progress.percent}%</p><div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-2"><Button asChild size="sm" variant="secondary"><Link to={`/workflow/production-line?run=${latestRun.id}`}>查看运行详情<ArrowRight /></Link></Button><Button asChild size="sm"><Link to="/workflow/incremental/correction">进入增量纠偏<ArrowRight /></Link></Button></div></> : <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">还没有增量运行。先导入素材包，再进入生产线。</p>}</aside>
        </section>
        <section className="rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-card py-card">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3"><div><p className="text-xs font-bold">本地接入合同状态</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">这里只统计模拟接入与本地素材包路由，不代表真实上游已连接。</p></div><Badge tone="neutral">content-ingress-v1 / v2</Badge></div>
          <div className="mt-4 grid gap-px bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-5">
            <IngressMetric label="已接收" value={ingressCounts.received} />
            <IngressMetric label="等待素材" value={ingressCounts.awaiting} />
            <IngressMetric label="已组包" value={ingressCounts.packaged} />
            <IngressMetric label="已阻塞/忽略" value={ingressCounts.blocked} />
            <IngressMetric label="重复复用" value={ingressCounts.duplicate} />
          </div>
        </section>
        <section className="rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4"><div><h2 className="visual-card-title">最近接入身份</h2><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">一级页只保留生产路由所需状态；候选复合键与证据进入二级抽屉。</p></div><Badge tone={recentContentRecords.some((item) => item.identity_status === "pending_verification") ? "warning" : "neutral"}>{recentContentRecords.filter((item) => item.identity_status === "pending_verification").length} 条身份待签认</Badge></div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.1fr)_140px_140px_150px_150px_auto] gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]"><span>来源 / 内容</span><span>类目</span><span>版本</span><span>身份状态</span><span>路由结果</span><span>操作</span></div>
          {recentContentRecords.map((record) => <IdentityRow key={record.id} record={record} onOpen={() => setSelectedIdentityId(record.id)} />)}
          {!recentContentRecords.length && !contentRecords.isFetching && <div className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">暂无内容接入记录。</div>}
        </section>
        <section className="rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-card py-card"><div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Play size={18} weight="fill" /><h2 className="visual-card-title">主线说明</h2></div><p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">自动 AI 迭代只负责生成完整候选机制和样本回归证据；是否启用候选、是否将正式标签事实发布给下游，仍由具备权限的人工分别决定。</p></section>
        </div></details>
      </div>
      <ContentIdentityDrawer record={selectedIdentity} verification={selectedVerification} open={selectedIdentity != null} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setSelectedIdentityId(null)} />
    </>
  )
}

function IdentityRow({ record, onOpen }: { record: ContentIdentityRecord; onOpen: () => void }) {
  const pending = record.identity_status === "pending_verification"
  const conflict = record.identity_status === "conflict"
  const statusLabel = pending ? "身份待签认" : conflict ? "身份冲突" : record.identity_status === "verified" ? "已签认" : "旧链路未签认"
  const routing = pending || conflict ? "阻断生产" : record.status === "ready" ? "可继续组包" : "等待素材"
  return <div className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.1fr)_140px_140px_150px_150px_auto] items-center gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4 text-xs last:border-0"><div><p className="font-bold">{record.source_system}</p><p className="mt-1 visual-code text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{record.content_id}</p></div><span>{record.category_key}</span><span>{record.content_version}</span><Badge tone={record.identity_status === "verified" ? "success" : pending || conflict ? "warning" : "neutral"}>{statusLabel}</Badge><span>{routing}</span><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={onOpen}>查看身份</Button></div>
}

function IngressMetric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: number }) {
  return <div className="bg-white px-4 py-3"><p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p><p className="mt-1 font-data text-lg font-bold">{value}</p></div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/pages/jobs-page.tsx =====
import {
  ArrowClockwise,
  CircleNotch,
  Pause,
  Play,
  WarningCircle,
  XCircle,
} from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { useState } from "react"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell"
import { AssetThumbnail } from "@/components/asset-thumbnail"
import { ImageLightbox, type ImagePreview } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Pager } from "@/components/ui/pager"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"
import { useAccess } from "@/lib/access"
import type { Paged } from "@/lib/paging"
import type { ActiveRunScope, Job, JobControl } from "@/lib/types"
import { formatPlatformTime } from "../lib/platform-time"

const PAGE_SIZE = 50

const stageLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  single: "单提示词完整评测",
  waiting: "等待领取",
  precheck: "分类与画质预检",
  aesthetic: "美感维度评测",
  aesthetic_repair: "维度评分校准",
  risk_review: "高风险结果复核",
  scoring: "外部评分计算",
  paused: "已暂停",
  canceled: "已取消",
  done: "已完成",
  error: "执行失败",
}

const statusLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  queued: "排队中",
  processing: "评测中",
  paused: "已暂停",
  canceled: "已取消",
  completed: "已完成",
  failed: "失败",
}

function statusTone(status: string) {
  if (status === "completed") return "success" as const
  if (status === "failed" || status === "canceled") return "danger" as const
  if (status === "processing") return "active" as const
  return "neutral" as const
}

export function JobsPage() {
  const canManage = useAccess().can("*")
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const [offset, setOffset] = useState(0)
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<ImagePreview | null>(null)
  const jobs = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["jobs", offset],
    queryFn: () => api<Paged<Job>>(`/api/jobs?limit=${PAGE_SIZE}&offset=${offset}`),
    // 翻到后面几页时保留上一页内容，避免每 2 秒轮询都把表格闪成空白
    placeholderData: (previous) => previous,
    refetchInterval: 2000,
  })
  const control = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["job-control"],
    queryFn: () => api<JobControl>("/api/jobs/control"),
    refetchInterval: 2000,
  })
  const action = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ name, payload }: {
      name: "pause" | "resume" | "cancel"
      payload?: { baseline_run_ids?: number[]; prompt_run_ids?: number[] }
    }) =>
      api<{ ok: boolean; affected: number; scope?: string }>(
        `/api/jobs/control/${name}`,
        payload
          ? { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(payload) }
          : { method: "POST" },
      ),
    onSuccess: async (data, { name }) => {
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["jobs"] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["job-control"] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["assets"] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["dashboard"] }),
      ])
      const message = name === "pause" ? "已暂停" : name === "resume" ? "已恢复" : "已取消"
      toast.success(`${message} ${data.affected} 个未完成任务`)
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const activeCount = control.data?.active_count ?? 0
  const activeRuns = control.data?.active_runs ?? []
  const isPaused = control.data?.paused ?? false

  function cancelRun(run: ActiveRunScope) {
    if (!canManage) return
    const label = run.kind === "baseline"
      ? `回归 #${run.run_id}（${run.category_key ?? ""} · ${run.created_by ?? ""}）`
      : `提示词回归 #${run.run_id}（${run.name ?? ""}）`
    if (
      window.confirm(
        `确定取消${label}吗？\n\n` +
          `已完成的 ${run.completed}/${run.total} 条结果会保留，` +
          `未完成的 ${run.active_jobs} 个任务会判失败，取消后不可继续。\n` +
          `其他正在进行的回归不受影响。`,
      )
    ) {
      action.mutate({
        name: "cancel",
        payload: run.kind === "baseline"
          ? { baseline_run_ids: [run.run_id] }
          : { prompt_run_ids: [run.run_id] },
      })
    }
  }

  function cancelAll() {
    if (!canManage) return
    // 这个按钮没有 run 作用域：它会把当时所有排队中与进行中的任务判失败，
    // 跨 run、跨基准集一并击穿（2026-08-23 run50-54、2026-08-27 run124 都是这样连废的）。
    // 所以要求输入「全部」二字确认，防止把它当成「停掉眼前这一轮」的快捷键。
    const runCount = activeRuns.length
    const answer = window.prompt(
      `⚠️ 全局取消：这会取消当前所有正在进行的回归` +
        `（${runCount > 0 ? `共 ${runCount} 轮、` : ""}${activeCount} 个未完成任务），不限于某一轮。\n\n` +
        `只想停某一轮，请取消此弹窗，改用上方对应回归行里的「取消这一轮」。\n\n` +
        `确认全局取消请输入：全部`,
    )
    if (answer === null) return
    if (answer.trim() !== "全部") {
      toast.error("未输入「全部」，已放弃全局取消")
      return
    }
    action.mutate({ name: "cancel" })
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        index="01.3"
        title="评测进度"
        description="查看素材排队、评测和完成进度。每次任务都会固定使用开始时的类目方案；暂停可继续，取消只结束未完成任务。"
        actions={
          <>
            {isPaused ? (
              <Button onClick={() => action.mutate({ name: "resume" })} disabled={!canManage || !activeCount || action.isPending}>
                <Play weight="fill" />恢复全部
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => action.mutate({ name: "pause" })} disabled={!canManage || !activeCount || action.isPending}>
                <Pause weight="fill" />暂停全部
              </Button>
            )}
            <Button variant="danger" onClick={cancelAll} disabled={!canManage || !activeCount || action.isPending}>
              <XCircle weight="bold" />取消全部
            </Button>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => Promise.all([jobs.refetch(), control.refetch()])}>
              <ArrowClockwise />刷新
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      />
      <div className="mx-auto shell-content min-w-0 px-page py-7 lg:py-10">
        <div className="mb-5 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          <span className="border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-3 py-2 rounded-md">排队 {control.data?.queued_count ?? 0}</span>
          <span className="border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-3 py-2 rounded-md">运行 {control.data?.processing_count ?? 0}</span>
          <span className="border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-3 py-2 rounded-md">暂停 {control.data?.paused_count ?? 0}</span>
        </div>
        {activeRuns.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mb-5 border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white rounded-md">
            <div className="border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">
              进行中的回归 · 取消只影响所选的那一轮
            </div>
            {activeRuns.map((run) => (
              <div
                key={`${run.kind}-${run.run_id}`}
                className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-3 last:border-0"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-sm">
                  <Badge tone="active">{run.kind === "baseline" ? "存量回归" : "提示词回归"}</Badge>
                  <span className="visual-code text-xs">#{run.run_id}</span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                    {run.kind === "baseline"
                      ? `${run.category_key ?? ""} · ${run.created_by ?? ""}`
                      : run.name ?? ""}
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                    进度 {run.completed}/{run.total} · 未完成 {run.active_jobs}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <Button
                  variant="secondary"
                  disabled={!canManage || action.isPending}
                  onClick={() => cancelRun(run)}
                  data-testid={`cancel-run-${run.kind}-${run.run_id}`}
                >
                  <XCircle weight="bold" />取消这一轮
                </Button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="overflow-x-auto visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white scrollbar-thin">
          {jobs.data?.items.length ? (
            <table className="w-full min-w-[1120px] border-collapse text-left text-sm">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                  <th className="px-4 py-3">任务</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-3">缩略图</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-3">图片</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-3">运行依据</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-3">阶段</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-3">进度</th>
                  <th className="px-3 py-3">最新更新时间</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3">状态</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {jobs.data.items.map((job) => (
                  <tr key={job.id} className="border-b border-[var(--line)] last:border-0">
                    <td className="visual-code px-4 py-4 text-xs">#{job.id.toString().padStart(5, "0")}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-4">
                      <AssetThumbnail assetId={job.asset_id} alt={job.asset_name} onPreview={setPreview} />
                    </td>
                    <td className="file-name max-w-[280px] truncate px-3 py-4">{job.asset_name}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-4">
                      <details className="visual-native-details">
                        <summary className="cursor-pointer text-xs font-semibold">类目方案已冻结</summary>
                        <div className="mt-2 border-l border-[var(--line-strong)] pl-3">
                          {job.prompt_version ? <p className="font-data text-xs">单次完整评测 · {job.prompt_version}</p> : <><p className="font-data text-xs">阶段 A · {job.prompt_a_version ?? "历史任务未记录"}</p><p className="font-data mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">阶段 B · {job.prompt_b_version ?? "历史任务未记录"}</p></>}
                          <p className="font-data mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">运行次数 {job.attempts}</p>
                        </div>
                      </details>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-4">{stageLabels[job.stage] ?? job.stage}</td>
                    <td className="px-3 py-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="h-1.5 w-36 bg-surface-sunken rounded-full"><div className="h-full w-full origin-left bg-active-border transition-transform duration-180 ease-[var(--visual-ease-out)] rounded-full" style={{ transform: `scaleX(${job.progress / 100})` }} /></div>
                        <span className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{job.progress}%</span>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="font-data whitespace-nowrap px-3 py-4 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{formatPlatformTime(job.updated_at)}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        {job.status === "processing" && <CircleNotch className="animate-spin" />}
                        {job.status === "failed" && <WarningCircle className="text-danger" />}
                        <Badge tone={statusTone(job.status)}>{statusLabels[job.status] ?? job.status}</Badge>
                      </div>
                      {job.error_message && <p className="mt-2 max-w-md text-xs leading-5 text-danger">{jobFailureText(job.error_message)}</p>}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          ) : offset > 0 ? (
            <div className="flex min-h-40 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 text-center">
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">这一页已经没有任务了，可能是列表在你翻页时缩短了。</p>
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setOffset(0)}>回到第一页</Button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex min-h-72 flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
              <CircleNotch size={30} weight="light" />
              <h2 className="visual-card-title mt-4">暂无逐条评测任务</h2>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">
                此表只列增量评测的逐条任务。存量回归（含真实模型评测）的进度请看上方「进行中的回归」，或到存量回归页查看分步详情。
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
          {jobs.data && jobs.data.total > 0 && (
            <div className="border-t border-[var(--line)]">
              <Pager
                page={jobs.data}
                fallbackLimit={PAGE_SIZE}
                onChange={setOffset}
                loading={jobs.isFetching}
                unit="个任务"
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      <ImageLightbox preview={preview} onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setPreview(null) }} />
    </>
  )
}

function jobFailureText(value: string) {
  const known = ({
    model_timeout: "模型响应超时，系统已停止本次处理",
    model_network: "模型服务网络异常",
    model_429: "模型服务当前繁忙",
    model_provider5xx: "模型服务暂时不可用",
    invalid_executor_output: "模型返回内容不完整，无法形成可信结果",
  } as Record<string, string>)[value]
  if (known) return known
  return /[\u3400-\u9fff]/u.test(value)
    ? value
    : "评测没有完成，请联系管理员查看运行记录"
}

      />
